# 題目目錄

依英文題名、英文描述、中文題名、中文詳細描述或演算法關鍵字按 `Ctrl+F` 搜尋。每列的檔案連結指向實際 C 解答；同題的多份解答優先列精選版本。

本表列出已有 C 原始碼的題目；原始碼是否完成或通過評測，仍須依個別檔案確認。LeetCode 尚未寫解答的完整英文題庫另見 [DB_INDEX.tsv](leetcode-c/DB_INDEX.tsv)。部分舊題僅有原始檔內的中英混合摘要，表格保留來源文字，詳細題意請點開對應檔案核對。英文題名若原題只有中文，使用便於搜尋的英文別名。

## LeetCode C 解答（1091 題）

| 英文題目名 | 英文描述 | 中文題目名 | 中文詳細描述 | 涵蓋的演算法範圍 | 檔案位置 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| LeetCode 1. Two Sum | Given an array of integers nums and an integer target, return indices of the two numbers such that they add up to target. You may assume that each input would have exactly one solution, and you may not use the same element twice. You can return the answer in any order. | 兩數之和 | 給定整數陣列 nums 與整數 target，請找出陣列中兩個相加等於 target 的元素，並回傳它們的索引。每筆輸入恰好有一組答案， 同一個元素不能重複使用；兩個索引的回傳順序不限。 | Array, Hash Table | [C](leetcode-c/easy/001_two-sum.c) |
| LeetCode 2. Add Two Numbers | You are given two non-empty linked lists representing two non-negative integers. The digits are stored in reverse order, and each of their nodes contains a single digit. Add the two numbers and return the sum as a linked list. You may assume the two numbers do not contain any leading zero, except the number 0 itself. | 兩數相加 | 給定兩條非空鏈結串列，分別表示兩個非負整數。每個節點只存一位數， 而且數字由個位數開始反向存放。請將兩數相加，並用相同的反向串列格式 回傳總和。除了數字 0 本身以外，輸入不會有前導零。 | Linked List, Math, Recursion | [C](leetcode-c/medium/002_add-two-numbers.c) |
| LeetCode 3. Longest Substring Without Repeating Characters | Given a string s, find the length of the longest substring without duplicate characters. | 無重複字元的最長子字串 | 給定字串 s，找出其中不含重複字元的最長「連續子字串」，並回傳長度。 | Hash Table, String, Sliding Window | [C](leetcode-c/medium/003_longest-substring-without-repeating-characters.c) |
| LeetCode 4. Median of Two Sorted Arrays | Given two sorted arrays nums1 and nums2 of size m and n respectively, return the median of the two sorted arrays. The overall run time complexity should be O(log (m+n)). | 尋找兩個正序數組的中位數 | 給定兩個長度分別為 m 與 n、皆依非遞減順序排列的整數陣列 nums1 與 nums2，請回傳兩個陣列合併後的中位數。演算法的整體時間複雜度 必須是 O(log(m+n))。 | Array, Binary Search, Divide and Conquer | [C](leetcode-c/hard/004_median-of-two-sorted-arrays.c) |
| LeetCode 5. Longest Palindromic Substring | Given a string s, return the longest palindromic substring in s. | 最長迴文子字串 | 給定字串 s，回傳其中最長的迴文子字串。迴文是由左向右與由右向左讀 都相同的字串；子字串必須由原字串中的連續字元組成。 | Two Pointers, String, Dynamic Programming | [C](leetcode-c/medium/005_longest-palindromic-substring.c) |
| LeetCode 6. Zigzag Conversion | The string "PAYPALISHIRING" is written in a zigzag pattern on a given number of rows like this: (you may want to display this pattern in a fixed font for better legibility) And then read line by line: "PAHNAPLSIIGYIR" Write the code that will take a string and make this conversion given a number of rows | Z 字形变换 | 原始檔未提供完整中文題意；請參閱連結中的英文題面。 | String | [C](leetcode-c/bulk/medium/006_zigzag-conversion.c) |
| LeetCode 7. Reverse Integer | Given a signed 32-bit integer x, return x with its digits reversed. If reversing x causes the value to go outside the signed 32-bit integer range [-231, 231 - 1], then return 0. Assume the environment does not allow you to store 64-bit integers (signed or unsigned). | 整數反轉 | 給定一個 32 位元有符號整數 x，將十進位數字的順序反轉後回傳。 若反轉結果超出 [-2^31, 2^31 - 1]，回傳 0。題目假設環境不能使用 64 位元整數來暫存結果。 | Math | [C](leetcode-c/medium/007_reverse-integer.c) |
| LeetCode 8. String to Integer (atoi) | Implement the myAtoi(string s) function, which converts a string to a 32-bit signed integer. A subarray is a contiguous non-empty sequence of elements within an array. | 子陣列的按位或 | 給定整數陣列 arr，計算所有非空連續子陣列的按位或結果中，有多少個相異值。單一元素子陣列的按位或即為該元素本身。 **思路**：程式維護所有以目前位置結尾之子陣列的相異 OR 值，將前一輪每個值與新元素 OR 後去重。另以全域雜湊表記錄所有出現過的結果並累加其數量。 | Array, Dynamic Programming, Bit Manipulation | [C](leetcode-c/bulk/medium/898_bitwise-ors-of-subarrays.c) |
| LeetCode 904. Fruit Into Baskets | You are visiting a farm that has a single row of fruit trees arranged from left to right. The trees are represented by an integer array fruits where fruits[i] is the type of fruit the ith tree produces. You want to collect as much fruit as possible. However, the owner has some strict rules that you must follow: Given the integer array… | 水果成籃 | 一排果樹由 fruits 表示，可任選起點後只能持續向右摘果。你有兩個籃子，每個籃子可裝無限多但只能裝一種水果；回傳最多可摘得的水果數量。 **思路**：程式以滑動視窗和計數陣列維護至多兩種水果。加入第三種水果時，從左端縮小視窗直到只剩兩種，並記錄最大視窗長度。 | Array, Hash Table, Sliding Window | [C](leetcode-c/bulk/medium/904_fruit-into-baskets.c) |
| LeetCode 905. Sort Array By Parity | Given an integer array nums, move all the even integers at the beginning of the array followed by all the odd integers. Return any array that satisfies this condition. | 依奇偶排序陣列 | 給定整數陣列 nums，將所有偶數移到陣列前方，所有奇數放在後方。回傳任一符合此條件的陣列即可，元素間的相對順序不受限制。 **思路**：程式從左側掃描，遇到奇數便與目前右側尾端元素交換，並縮小右側界線。如此會將偶數留在前段、奇數逐步推往後段。 | Array, Two Pointers, Sorting | [C](leetcode-c/bulk/easy/905_sort-array-by-parity.c) |
| LeetCode 907. Sum of Subarray Minimums | Given an array of integers arr, find the sum of min(b), where b ranges over every (contiguous) subarray of arr. Since the answer may be large, return the answer modulo 109 + 7. | 子陣列最小值之和 | 給定整數陣列 arr，對每個非空連續子陣列取其最小值並加總。由於結果可能很大，回傳結果對 10⁹+7 取模後的值。 **思路**：程式使用單調遞增索引堆疊，計算每個位置作為結尾時所有子陣列最小值的總和。遇到較小或相等元素便彈出堆疊，利用前一個較小元素與距離更新貢獻，再累加到答案。 | Array, Dynamic Programming, Stack, Monotonic Stack | [C](leetcode-c/bulk/medium/907_sum-of-subarray-minimums.c) |
| LeetCode 908. Smallest Range I | You are given an integer array nums and an integer k. In one operation, you can choose any index i where 0 <= i < nums.length and change nums[i] to nums[i] + x where x is an integer from the range [-k, k]. You can apply this operation at most once for each index i. The score of nums is the difference between the maximum and minimum… | 最小範圍 I | 對 nums 的每個元素最多可操作一次，將其加上一個介於 −k 與 k 的整數。分數定義為最大值與最小值之差，回傳操作後可得到的最小分數。 **思路**：程式掃描取得原陣列最小值與最大值，分別向內調整 k。若調整後區間交疊則答案為 0，否則回傳 max−min−2k。 | Array, Math | [C](leetcode-c/bulk/easy/908_smallest-range-i.c) |
| LeetCode 909. Snakes and Ladders | You are given an n x n integer matrix board where the cells are labeled from 1 to n2 in a Boustrophedon style starting from the bottom left of the board (i.e. board[n - 1][0]) and alternating direction each row. You start on square 1 of the board. In each move, starting from square curr, do the following: A board square on row r and… | 蛇梯棋 | 給定 n × n 棋盤，方格從左下角的 1 起按蛇形編號至 n²，且每列方向交替。從 1 出發時，每次擲骰可前進 1 到 6 格；落在有蛇或梯子的格子必須立即移至其指定終點，但同一次擲骰不會繼續連鎖移動。求抵達 n² 的最少擲骰次數，若無法抵達則回傳 -1。 **思路**：將格號換算為棋盤座標後，以 BFS 逐層枚舉每次可走的六個目的地；入隊前套用至多一次蛇或梯子，並以走訪標記避免重複搜尋。 | Array, Breadth-First Search, Matrix | [C](leetcode-c/bulk/medium/909_snakes-and-ladders.c) |
| LeetCode 912. Sort an Array | Given an array of integers nums, sort the array in ascending order and return it. | LeetCode 913. Cat and Mouse | A game on an undirected graph is played by two players, Mouse and Cat, who alternate turns. ## UVa C 解答（1024 題）

| 英文題目名 | 英文描述 | 中文題目名 | 中文詳細描述 | 涵蓋的演算法範圍 | 檔案位置 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| UVa 100. The 3n + 1 Problem | Given two integers 'i' and 'j', examine every integer in the inclusive interval between them. Repeatedly replace an even value by half of itself and an odd value by '3n + 1', count the terms through 1, and output the original pair followed by the largest cycle length found. | 3n + 1 問題（考拉茲猜想） | 每行輸入兩個整數 'i'、'j'；對兩者之間（含端點）的每個整數產生考拉茲數列：偶數除以 2，奇數變成 '3n + 1'，直到 1 為止。計算各數列包含起點與 1 的項數，輸出原始順序的 'i j'，以及區間內最大的循環長度。 | simulation, math, collatz-conjecture | [C](UVa-c/volume001/100_the-3n-plus-1-problem.c) |
| UVa 101. The Blocks Problem | Start with block 'i' alone at position 'i'. Process 'move' or 'pile' commands combined with 'onto' or 'over': blocks above selected blocks may be returned home, and either one block or an entire pile is transferred. Ignore commands involving blocks already in the same stack, stop at 'quit', and print every final stack. | 積木問題 | 輸入積木數量後，每塊編號 'i' 的積木一開始單獨放在第 'i' 疊。依序執行 'move/pile' 與 'onto/over' 組成的四種指令；其中可能先把目標上方積木送回原位，再搬動單塊或整段積木。兩塊已在同一疊時忽略該指令，讀到 'quit' 後輸出每個位置的最終堆疊。 | simulation, data-structures | [C](UVa-c/volume001/101_the-blocks-problem.c) |
| UVa 102. Ecological Bin Packing | Each input case gives the counts of brown, green, and clear bottles in three bins. Assign one distinct color to each bin and move all bottles of the other colors out. Output the three-letter assignment requiring the fewest moves, choosing the lexicographically earliest assignment on a tie, followed by the move count. | 生態回收桶 | 每筆輸入給出三個箱子中棕色、綠色、透明瓶子的數量。要讓每個箱子只保留一種顏色且三色各占一箱，計算六種顏色排列所需搬走的瓶子數。輸出搬移次數最少的三字母配置與次數；若同樣少，取字典序最前的配置。 | math, greedy, simulation | [C](UVa-c/volume001/102_ecological-bin-packing.c) |
| UVa 103. Stacking Boxes | Given k n-dimensional boxes, find the longest sequence of nested boxes. | 多維箱子巢狀堆疊 | 給定 k 個 n 維箱子，判定維度排序後能否完全嵌套，求最長嵌套序列 (DAG 最長路 / LIS)。 | dynamic-programming, dag, sorting, lis | [C](UVa-c/volume001/103_stacking-boxes.c) |
| UVa 104. Arbitrage | Find minimum length sequence of currency exchanges that yields > 1.01 profit. | 套匯獲利鏈 | 尋找貨幣兌換的最短交易鏈，使兌換回到原貨幣後獲利超過 1% (乘積 > 1.01)。 | graph, floyd-warshall, dynamic-programming | [C](UVa-c/volume001/104_arbitrage.c) |
| UVa 105. The Skyline Problem | Buildings are supplied as triples '(L, H, R)' describing their left coordinate, height, and right coordinate. Combine all buildings and output the critical x-coordinates where the visible maximum height changes, alternating each coordinate with its new skyline height and ending when the height returns to zero. | 天際線輪廓問題 | 輸入若干棟以左界 'L'、高度 'H'、右界 'R' 表示的建築。對每個水平座標取所有覆蓋建築的最大高度，找出整體輪廓高度發生變化的位置。輸出一連串「座標、新高度」關鍵點，最後包含天際線降回 0 的位置。 | simulation, array | [C](UVa-c/volume001/105_the-skyline-problem.c) |
| UVa 106. Fermat vs. Pythagoras | Count primitive Pythagorean triples with z <= N, and count numbers <= N not in any triple. | 費馬對畢氏 (互質畢氏三元數) | 計算斜邊 z <= N 的本原畢氏三元組數量，以及在 1..N 中未包含在任何畢氏三元組中的整數數量。 | math, number-theory, pythagorean-triples | [C](UVa-c/volume001/106_fermat-vs-pythagoras.c) |
| UVa 107. The Cat in the Hat | Initial cat height H, final 1-height cats W. Each cat has N cats of height H/(N+1). Find non-working cats and total height. | 戴帽子的貓 | 初始貓高 H，最後高度為 1 的貓有 W 隻。每隻貓帽子裡有 N 隻高 H/(N+1) 的小貓。求不工作的貓數量與總高度。 | math, binary-search, logarithms | [C](UVa-c/volume001/107_the-cat-in-the-hat.c) |
| UVa 108. Maximum Sum | Given an 'N × N' integer matrix, choose a nonempty axis-aligned rectangular submatrix whose entries have the greatest possible sum. Output that maximum sum, including the correct negative value when every matrix entry is negative. | 最大二維子矩陣和 | 輸入一個 'N × N' 整數矩陣，從中選擇非空且邊界平行於矩陣的矩形區域，使區域內元素總和最大。程式枚舉上下邊界、將各欄壓成一維後套用最大子陣列演算法，最後輸出最大總和；全為負數時也要保留正確的負值答案。 | dynamic-programming, 2d-kadane | [C](UVa-c/volume001/108_maximum-sum.c) |
| UVa 109. SCUD Busters | Given kingdom missile defense sites, find convex hull polygons of each kingdom. If a missile hits inside a kingdom, its power is destroyed. Calculate total area destroyed. | 飛彈防禦多邊形凸包與面積計算 | 給定各王國的飛彈防禦基地座標，求各王國的凸包多邊形。若敵方飛彈擊中某王國凸包內部，該王國防禦網失效，計算所有失效王國之凸包總面積。 | geometry, convex-hull | [C](UVa-c/volume001/109_scud-busters.c) |
| UVa 110. Meta-Loopless Sorts | Generate a Pascal program with nested if-else to sort n variables without loops. | 無迴圈排序程式碼生成器 | 以遞迴回溯生成包含完整 if-else 決策樹的 Pascal 無迴圈排序程式碼。 | backtracking, recursion, code-generation | [C](UVa-c/volume001/110_meta-loopless-sorts.c) |
| UVa 111. History Grading | Given correct chronological rank of events and students' rankings, find length of longest valid relative order. | 歷史事件排序評分 | 給定歷史事件的正確年代順序與學生的排序回答，計算學生保留的相對年代順序的最長長度 (LIS / LCS)。 | dynamic-programming, lcs, lis | [C](UVa-c/volume001/111_history-grading.c) |
| UVa 112. Tree Summing | Given target sum and S-expression binary tree, determine if there exists root-to-leaf path with this sum. | 二元樹路徑和 | 解析括號 S-expression 表達的二元樹，判定是否存在一條從根結點到葉節點的路徑和等於目標值。 | recursion, parsing, tree | [C](UVa-c/volume001/112_tree-summing.c) |
| UVa 113. Power of Cryptography | For each pair 'n' and 'p', the input guarantees that 'p = k^n' for an integer 'k'. Recover and output 'k', even though the decimal representation of 'p' may be very large. | 密碼學的威力 | 每筆輸入先給指數 'n'，再給一個很大的整數 'p'，並保證存在整數 'k' 使 'p = k^n'。題目要求反推出這個整數底數；實作以浮點數計算 'n' 次方根並四捨五入，逐筆輸出 'k'。 | math | [C](UVa-c/volume001/113_power-of-cryptography.c) |
| UVa 114. Simulation Wizardry | Simulate pinball movement with bumpers, wall rebound, lifetime decay, and score points. | 彈珠檯魔法模擬 | 模擬彈珠檯上彈珠碰到緩衝障礙物、牆壁反彈、方向右轉、壽命遞減與得分的物理規則。 | simulation, 2d-grid | [C](UVa-c/volume001/114_simulation-wizardry.c) |
| UVa 115. Climbing Trees | Given parent-child relationships, determine the relationship between two individuals: child, parent, sibling, cousin with removal, etc. | 家譜樹血緣親屬關係判定 | 給定若干對父子/母子血緣關係，判斷任意兩人之間的親屬關係：如直系尊親屬/卑親屬、兄弟姊妹或具有代差的堂表親屬。 | tree, dfs, lca | [C](UVa-c/volume001/115_climbing-trees.c) |
| UVa 116. Unidirectional TSP | Find path from column 1 to n in a cylindrical grid with minimum weight and lexicographically smallest row sequence. | 單向旅行商問題 (循環網格 DP) | 在上下相通的圓柱網格中，由左至右移動，求累加權重最小且字典序最小的列移動路徑。 | dynamic-programming, grid, lexicographical | [C](UVa-c/volume001/116_unidirectional-tsp.c) |
| UVa 117. The Postal Worker Rings Once | Find minimum distance to traverse all edges in connected undirected graph (Chinese Postman Problem). | 郵差總按一次鈴 (中國郵差問題) | 在連通無向圖中尋找遍歷所有邊至少一次的最短閉迴路 (中國郵差問題)。 | graph, eulerian-path, floyd-warshall, shortest-path | [C](UVa-c/volume001/117_the-postal-worker-rings-once.c) |
| UVa 118. Mutant Flatworld Explorers | A rectangular world and a sequence of robots are given. Each robot starts at a coordinate and orientation, then follows left turns, right turns, and forward moves. A robot that steps beyond the border is lost and leaves a scent at its last valid position; later robots ignore a forward command that would fall from the same scented edge.… | 異變的扁平世界探險家 | 先輸入矩形世界的右上邊界，接著逐一給定機器人的起點、朝向與由 'L'、'R'、'F' 組成的指令。機器人若向前走出邊界便在最後合法位置留下氣味並標為 'LOST'；之後其他機器人在同一位置、同一掉落方向會忽略該前進指令。輸出每台機器人的最終座標、方向及是否失蹤。 | simulation, grid | [C](UVa-c/volume001/118_mutant-flatworld-explorers.c) |
| UVa 119. Greedy Gift Givers | A group of named friends exchanges gifts. For each giver, divide the stated amount equally among the listed recipients; any indivisible remainder stays with the giver. Output every person's net gain or loss in the same order in which the names were introduced. | 貪婪的送禮者 | 每組資料先給人數與依序出現的姓名，再描述每位送禮者準備的金額及收禮名單。金額以整數平均分給收禮者，不能整除的餘額由送禮者保留；沒有人可送時也不扣款。累計每人的收入減支出，最後按原姓名順序輸出淨收支。 | simulation, string | [C](UVa-c/volume001/119_greedy-gift-givers.c) |
| UVa 120. Stacks of Flapjacks | Each input line lists pancake diameters from the top of a stack to the bottom. A flip inserts a spatula at some depth and reverses all pancakes above it. Echo the original stack, then output a sequence of flip positions that sorts it with the largest pancake at the bottom, terminated by '0'. | 煎餅堆排序 | 每行輸入一疊煎餅的直徑，順序由頂端到最底端。一次操作可選擇某個深度，將其上方整段煎餅反轉；程式反覆把尚未定位的最大煎餅先翻到頂端，再翻到目前底部。先原樣輸入該列，再輸出完成排序的翻轉位置序列，並以 '0' 結束。 | sorting, simulation, greedy | [C](UVa-c/volume001/120_stacks-of-flapjacks.c) |
| UVa 121. Pipe Fitters | Given grid dimensions a x b, calculate maximum number of unit pipes packed using standard grid vs skew (hexagonal) packing. | 網格與交錯排管幾何堆疊 | 給定容器長寬 a x b，計算單位圓管以標準正交網格排列與交錯蜂巢排列下最多可容納之管子數量。 | geometry, greedy | [C](UVa-c/volume001/121_pipe-fitters.c) |
| UVa 122. Trees on the level | Given node values and binary tree paths (e.g. 5,LL), verify complete tree and output level-order traversal. | 二元樹層次遍歷 | 給定二元樹各結點數值與左右路徑標記，驗證樹是否結構完整無重複無懸空結點，並輸出由上至下的層次走訪。 | binary-tree, bfs, parsing | [C](UVa-c/volume001/122_trees-on-the-level.c) |
| UVa 123. Searching Quickly | Generate a Key Word In Context (KWIC) index: alphabetized list of titles rotated around capitalized non-stop words. | 關鍵字上下文索引 (KWIC Index) | 生成關鍵字上下文索引 (KWIC)：忽略停用詞，將每個非停用詞大寫並將其排在關鍵字位置，按字典序穩定排序。 | string, sorting, simulation | [C](UVa-c/volume001/123_searching-quickly.c) |
| UVa 124. Following Orders | Given a set of variables and ordering constraints, print all valid topological orderings in alphabetical order. | 依順序排程 (全排列拓撲排序) | 給定一組變數及其先後依序限制條件，按字母順序輸出所有合法的拓撲排序序列。 | graph, topological-sort, backtracking | [C](UVa-c/volume001/124_following-orders.c) |
| UVa 125. Numbering Paths | Given directed street network, find the number of paths between all pairs of intersections, or -1 if infinitely many paths exist (via cycle). | 有向圖路徑計數與環路判定 | 給定有向街道網路，計算所有交會點對之間的路徑數量；若兩點間存在有向環導致有無窮多條路徑，則標記為 -1。 | graph, floyd-warshall | [C](UVa-c/volume001/125_numbering-paths.c) |
| UVa 126. The Pascal Library | Multiply two multivariate polynomials in variables x and y, combine like terms, and format output with exponents on the line above. | 帕斯卡多項式乘法與美觀輸出 | 計算二元多項式乘法 (含 x, y 變數)，同類項合併化簡，並以上下兩行排版輸出多項式指數。 | string, simulation, math | [C](UVa-c/volume001/126_the-pascal-library.c) |
| UVa 127. "Accordian" Patience | Simulate 52-card Accordian patience game: move card onto left 1st or 3rd pile if same suit or rank. | 手風琴接龍紙牌模擬 | 模擬 52 張紙牌手風琴接龍：若與左邊第 1 疊或第 3 疊花色或點數相同，優先將牌堆向左疊合。 | simulation, stack, card-game | [C](UVa-c/volume001/127_accordian-patience.c) |
| UVa 128. Software CRC | Compute 2-byte CRC checksum with generator polynomial g = 34943 such that message * 2^16 + CRC == 0 (mod g). | 軟體循環冗餘校驗碼 (CRC-16) | 計算 2 位元組 CRC 校驗碼，使得字串 ASCII 位元流左移 16 位元加上 CRC 碼後可被 34943 整除。 | math, bit-manipulation, crc | [C](UVa-c/volume001/128_software-crc.c) |
| UVa 129. Krypton Factor | A string is 'hard' if it contains no two adjacent identical substrings. Given n and alphabet size L, find the n-th hard string in lexicographic order. | 無連續相鄰重複子字串迴溯法 | 若一個字串中不存在任意兩個連續且完全相同的子字串，則稱其為「困難字串」。給定整數 n 與字元集大小 L，求字典序第 n 個困難字串。 | backtracking, string | [C](UVa-c/volume001/129_krypton-factor.c) |
| UVa 130. Roman Roulette | Josephus variant: the k-th person eliminated is replaced by the next k-th person. Find starting position for survivor 1. | 羅馬輪盤 (約瑟夫環換位變形) | 約瑟夫環變形：第 k 個被數到的人被處決後，由接下來數到的第 k 個人替補其位置。求使 1 號存活的起始者位置。 | simulation, josephus | [C](UVa-c/volume001/130_roman-roulette.c) |
| UVa 131. The Hands of Poker | Given a hand of 5 cards and a deck of 5 cards, choose a subset of hand to discard and draw from deck in order to achieve the highest possible poker hand rank. | 五張換牌最佳撲克牌型評估 | 給定手中的 5 張手牌與牌堆頂部的 5 張牌，可從手牌中選擇任意張丟棄，並從牌堆頂依序補齊至 5 張，求可組成的最高撲克牌型。 | simulation, brute-force | [C](UVa-c/volume001/131_the-hands-of-poker.c) |

| UVa 134. Loglan - A Logical Language | Determine whether a given Loglan sentence conforms to the specified grammar rules (A, MOD, DA, PA, etc.). | 人工語言語法分析詞性判定 | 根據 Loglan 邏輯人工語言的詞性分類規則 (母音結尾為名詞、輔音母音分佈) 檢驗給定句子文法結構是否合法。 | string, parsing | [C](UVa-c/volume001/134_loglan-a-logical-language.c) |
| UVa 135. No Rectangles | Given k, construct a k-regular incidence structure of size k*(k-1)+1 containing no 4-cycles (rectangles in bipartite graph). | 有限射影幾何與無矩形點集構造 | 給定每列包含 k 個點的要求，利用有限射影平面構造點線關聯矩陣，使得任意兩行無兩個相同的公共列 (無矩形環路)。 | math, combinatorics | [C](UVa-c/volume001/135_no-rectangles.c) |
| UVa 136. Ugly Numbers | Ugly numbers are positive integers whose only prime factors are 2, 3, and 5, with 1 included as the first ugly number. Generate the sequence in increasing order and print the fixed requested value: the 1500th ugly number. | 醜數 | 醜數定義為質因數只可能含 2、3、5 的正整數，並把 1 視為第一個醜數。本題沒有一般資料輸入；程式用三個指標合併乘 2、乘 3、乘 5 形成的遞增序列，去除重複值後輸出第 1500 個醜數。 | math, dynamic-programming, three-pointers | [C](UVa-c/volume001/136_ugly-numbers.c) |
| UVa 137. Polygons | Given two convex polygons, compute the total area of both polygons minus twice the area of their intersection. | 兩凸多邊形交集與聯集面積幾何 | 給定兩個凸多邊形，計算兩多邊形聯集面積扣除交集面積 (即對稱差面積)。 | geometry, polygon | [C](UVa-c/volume001/137_polygons.c) |
| UVa 138. Street Numbers | Find 10 pairs (x, n) such that sum 1..(x-1) == sum (x+1)..n. | 門牌號碼平衡數 (佩爾方程) | 求前 10 組正整數 (x, n)，滿足 1 到 x-1 的和等於 x+1 到 n 的和 (佩爾方程 8x^2 + 1 = y^2)。 | math, number-theory, pell-equation | [C](UVa-c/volume001/138_street-numbers.c) |

| UVa 143. Orchard Trees | Given vertices of a triangle, count how many integer grid points (trees) with 1 <= x, y <= 99 fall inside or on the boundary of the triangle. | 果園三角形內部網格點個數幾何計數 | 給定果園內三角形邊界的頂點座標，統計有多少個座標為整數且範圍在 1 <= x, y <= 99 內的樹木 (網格點) 落在三角形內部或邊界上。 | geometry, lattice-points | [C](UVa-c/volume001/143_orchard-trees.c) |

Gondwanaland Telecom | Given call start and end times and rate category, calculate minutes spent in day (8am-6pm), evening (6pm-10pm), and night (10pm-8am) bands, and total bill. | 跨時段電話通話費率分段累計 | 給定電話通話起訖時間與費率代碼，分段統計日間 (8:00-18:00)、傍晚 (18:00-22:00) 與夜間 (22:00-8:00) 的通話分鐘數與總話費。 | simulation, math | [C](UVa-c/volume001/145_gondwanaland-telecom.c) |

Print that immediate successor, or print 'No Successor' when the input is already the greatest permutation. Input ends at '#'. | 識別碼字典序下一個排列 | 每行輸入一個識別碼字串，要求用相同字元重新排列，找出字典序中緊接在原字串之後的排列。由右向左找可增加的位置、交換後反轉後綴；若原字串已是最大排列則輸出 'No Successor'。讀到 '#' 時結束。 | string, permutation | [C](UVa-c/volume001/146_id-codes.c) |
| UVa 147. Dollars | Given dollar amount up to $300.00, count ways to make change using 11 predefined coin and bill values. | 貨幣換幣組合數 | 給定最多 $300.00 的金額，計算使用 11 種特定硬幣與鈔票面額湊齊金額的方法數 (完全背包 DP)。 | dynamic-programming, coin-change | [C](UVa-c/volume001/147_dollars.c) |
| UVa 148. Anagram Checker | Given a dictionary of words and a phrase, find all anagrams of the phrase made up of words from the dictionary, excluding the phrase itself. | 字謎組合詞庫字典比對回溯 | 給定英文字典與一段短語，以回溯法找出所有由字典中單詞組成的字母異位詞 (Anagram) 短語，且不得與原始短語單詞完全一致。 | backtracking, string | [C](UVa-c/volume001/148_anagram-checker.c) |

| UVa 150. Double Time | Convert dates and weekdays between Old Style (Julian calendar) and New Style (Gregorian calendar). | 儒略曆與格里高利曆雙向精準轉換 | 精確計算儒略曆 (舊曆) 與格里高利曆 (新曆) 之間的曆法轉換與星期對齊。 | simulation, math, date | [C](UVa-c/volume001/150_double-time.c) |
| UVa 151. Power Crisis | Regions numbered '1' through 'N' are switched off in a cyclic elimination process. Region 1 must be removed first, and every 'm'-th remaining region is then removed. For each 'N', find the smallest positive 'm' that leaves region 13 as the final powered region; input ends with zero. | 電力危機 | 對每個輸入的地區數 'N'，編號 1 的地區先停電，之後在仍有電的地區間循環計數，每數到第 'm' 個便使其停電。枚舉正整數 'm' 並模擬約瑟夫淘汰過程，找出讓第 13 區最後才停電的最小 'm'；輸入 0 結束，逐筆輸出答案。 | simulation, math, josephus | [C](UVa-c/volume001/151_power-crisis.c) |

| UVa 153. Permalex | Given a string with potential duplicate letters, compute its 1-based lexicographical permutation rank. | 字典序排列位次 | 給定一個可能含有重複字母的字串，計算其在所有相異字母排列中的 1-based 字典序排名。 | combinatorics, math, string | [C](UVa-c/volume001/153_permalex.c) |
| UVa 154. Recycling | Given recycling bin allocation schemes across multiple cities, find the city whose scheme requires the minimum total changes to match others. | 垃圾回收站相異屬性最少換算投票 | 給定多座城市五種垃圾桶顏色與回收類型的配置，找出與其他所有城市相異配置最少 (即共識度最高) 的城市編號。 | brute-force, string | [C](UVa-c/volume001/154_recycling.c) |
| UVa 155. All Squares | Count how many nested recursive squares cover a given point (px, py). | 遞迴正方形覆蓋點計數 | 遞迴繪製四角中心的正方形，計算目標點 (px, py) 被多少個正方形所覆蓋 (遞迴幾何)。 | recursion, geometry | [C](UVa-c/volume001/155_all-squares.c) |

| UVa 160. Factors and Factorials | For each positive integer 'N', express 'N!' by listing the exponent of every prime not exceeding 'N', in ascending prime order and in the required aligned format. Paper Folding | Given n folds of a strip of paper, simulate unfolding at 90 degree angles (dragon curve) and render the ASCII line representation. | 對折紙張龍形曲線展開繪製 | 給定紙帶連續向右對折 n 次後的展開過程，模擬每次展開 90 度的龍形曲線 (Dragon Curve)，並排版繪製出 ASCII 字元曲線。 | simulation, recursion | [C](UVa-c/volume001/177_paper-folding.c) |

Message Decoding | Decode a binary message where headers define characters corresponding to binary codes of increasing length (0, 1; 00, 01, 10; etc.). | 二進位編碼解碼 | 解碼二進位訊息：首行標頭依長度對應二進位碼（長度 1: 0；長度 2: 00, 01, 10；長度 3: 000..110，全 1 為長度終止符）。 | string, bit-manipulation, decoding | [C](UVa-c/volume002/213_message-decoding.c) |

| UVa 343. What Base Is This? | Find smallest bases B1 and B2 (2 <= B <= 36) such that string X in base B1 equals string Y in base B2. | 這是幾進位？(任意進位等值搜尋) | 尋找最小進位 B1 與 B2 (2 <= B <= 36)，使得字串 X 在 B1 進位下的數值等於字串 Y 在 B2 進位下的數值。 | math, base-conversion, brute-force | [C](UVa-c/volume003/343_what-base-is-this.c) |
| UVa 344. Roman Digititis | Count cumulative total occurrences of i, v, x, l, c across all page numbers 1 through N written in Roman numerals. | 羅馬數字字母頻率統計 | 統計 1 到 N 所有頁碼寫成羅馬數字時，字母 i, v, x, l, c 累計出現的總次數 (N <= 100)。 | simulation, string, math | [C](UVa-c/volume003/344_roman-digititis.c) |
| UVa 348. Optimal Array Multiplication Sequence | Find optimal parenthesization of matrix chain multiplication to minimize scalar multiplications. | 最佳矩陣鏈乘積括號化 | 求矩陣鏈相乘的最佳括號化方案，使純量乘法運算次數最小 (經典矩陣鏈乘積區間 DP)。 | dynamic-programming, matrix-chain-multiplication | [C](UVa-c/volume003/348_optimal-array-multiplication-sequence.c) |
| UVa 350. Pseudo-Random Numbers | A linear congruential generator produces 'x(next) = (Z × x + I) mod M' from an initial value 'L'. For each quadruple 'Z I M L', determine the length of the repeating cycle and print it with the case number. Four zeroes terminate the input. | 偽隨機數生成週期 | 每筆輸入乘數 'Z'、增量 'I'、模數 'M' 與初值 'L'，依 '下一項 = (Z × 當前項 + I) mod M' 產生序列。記錄每個值第一次出現的步數，某值再次出現時，兩次步數差就是循環節長度；輸出案例編號與長度，四個 0 結束。 | math, simulation, floyd-cycle | [C](UVa-c/volume003/350_pseudo-random-numbers.c) |
| UVa 352. The Seasonal War | Count number of war eagles (connected components of 1s in 8 directions) in n x n binary image. | 季節戰爭 (八方向連通鷹眼連通塊) | 在 n x n 的二值圖像中，計算 8 方向連通的作戰鷹群 (數值 1 形成的連通塊) 總數量。 | graph, flood-fill, dfs | [C](UVa-c/volume003/352_the-seasonal-war.c) |
| UVa 353. Pesky Palindromes | Count total number of unique palindromic substrings in a given string. | 討厭的回文 (相異回文子字串計數) | 計算給定字串中所有不重複的相異回文子字串總數。 | string, palindrome, brute-force | [C](UVa-c/volume003/353_pesky-palindromes.c) |
| UVa 355. The Bases Are Loaded | Convert a number from base B1 to base B2 (2 <= B1, B2 <= 16), detecting invalid input digits. | 基底全滿 (進位轉換驗證) | 驗證字串是否為合法 B1 進位數，並將其正確轉換為 B2 進位表示法 (2 <= B1, B2 <= 16)。 | math, base-conversion, string | [C](UVa-c/volume003/355_the-bases-are-loaded.c) |
| UVa 356. Square Pegs And Round Holes | Given radius R = (2n - 1) / 2, count the number of 1x1 cells completely inside the circle and cells intersected by circle boundary. | 方釘圓孔 (圓與網格完全包含及穿過計數) | 半徑為 R = (2n - 1) / 2 的圓覆蓋網格，求完全落在圓內的 1x1 網格數以及被圓周切割的網格數。 | geometry, math | [C](UVa-c/volume003/356_square-pegs-and-round-holes.c) |
| UVa 357. Let Me Count The Ways | For each amount in cents, count the unordered ways to make exactly that amount using unlimited U.S. coins of 1, 5, 10, 25, and 50 cents. Print the grammatically correct singular or plural sentence with the computed count. | 算算有多少種方法 | 每行輸入一個以分為單位的金額，可不限枚數使用 1、5、10、25、50 分硬幣，計算組合而非排列的找零方法數。以完全背包依硬幣順序更新方法數，逐筆輸出答案，並依答案是否為 1 選用題目指定的單複數句型。 | dynamic-programming, coin-change | [C](UVa-c/volume003/357_let-me-count-the-ways.c) |
| UVa 369. Combinations | Compute exact combination C(N, M) for N <= 100 without intermediate 64-bit overflow. | 組合數精確計算 (防止溢位的 C(N, M)) | 精確計算二項式係數 C(N, M) = N! / (M! * (N-M)!)，避免乘法中途發生數值溢位。 | math, combinatorics | [C](UVa-c/volume003/369_combinations.c) |
| UVa 371. Ackermann Functions | Find integer in range [L, H] that produces the longest Collatz sequence (where f(1) continues to 4-2-1, len >= 3). | 考拉茲序列極大值點 | 尋找區間 [L, H] 內產生最長 3n+1 序列長度的整數 (注意 n=1 時依題目定義長度為 3)。 | simulation, math, collatz | [C](UVa-c/volume003/371_ackermann-functions.c) |
| UVa 374. Big Mod | Repeatedly read integers 'B', 'P', and 'M', and compute 'B^P mod M' without constructing the enormous power itself. Print one modular result for each input triple. | 大數快速冪模運算 | 每組輸入底數 'B'、指數 'P' 與模數 'M'，要求輸出 'B^P mod M'。利用指數的二進位分解反覆平方，每當目前位元為 1 就把該冪乘入答案，所有乘法過程立即取模，因此不用真的建立龐大的 'B^P'。 | math, number-theory, modular-exponentiation | [C](UVa-c/volume003/374_big-mod.c) |
| UVa 377. Cowmulting | Simulate a base-4 arithmetic processor with symbols 'V'=0, 'U'=1, 'C'=2, 'D'=3 and operations A, S, R, N. | 牛之四進制運算器 | 模擬以四進制字元 'V'=0, 'U'=1, 'C'=2, 'D'=3 表示的 8 位數運算器，支援加法、左移、右移與空操作。 | simulation, base-conversion, math | [C](UVa-c/volume003/377_cowmulting.c) |
| UVa 378. Intersecting Lines | Given two 2D lines defined by points, determine if they are identical (LINE), parallel (NONE), or intersect (POINT x y). | 直線相交判斷與交點計算 | 給定二維平面上的兩條直線，判定兩線是否重合 (LINE)、平行 (NONE) 或相交於單一點 (POINT x y)。 | geometry, math, linear-equations | [C](UVa-c/volume003/378_intersecting-lines.c) |
| UVa 382. Perfection | For each positive integer, sum all of its proper positive divisors. Classify the number as 'PERFECT' when the sum equals it, 'DEFICIENT' when the sum is smaller, or 'ABUNDANT' when the sum is larger, and print the classifications in the required report format. Input ends with zero. | 完美數、虧數與盈數判定 | 逐筆讀入正整數，列舉並加總其所有不含自身的正因數。因數和等於原數時分類為 'PERFECT'，小於原數為 'DEFICIENT'，大於原數為 'ABUNDANT'；依題目規定先後印出報表標頭、每個數的對齊結果與結尾，輸入 0 時停止。 | math, number-theory | [C](UVa-c/volume003/382_perfection.c) |
| UVa 383. Shipping Routes | Given a network of warehouse shipping legs, find the shortest path in legs between source and destination and calculate cost ($100 * size * legs). | 貨物航線 | 給定倉庫間的航線網絡，求起點到終點的最短航線段數，並計算運輸成本（$100 * 貨物大小 * 段數）。 | graph, bfs, shortest-path | [C](UVa-c/volume003/383_shipping-routes.c) |
| UVa 384. Slurmys | Determine if a string is a Slurpy, which consists of a Slimp followed by a Slump. | 外星字串文法解析 (Slump, Slimp, Slurpy) | 解析特定外星文法：判斷字串是否為 Slurpy（由一個 Slimp 後接一個 Slump 組成）。 | string, grammar, parsing | [C](UVa-c/volume003/384_slurmys.c) |
| UVa 386. Perfect Cubes | Find all integer quadruples 2 <= b <= c <= d <= a <= 200 such that a^3 = b^3 + c^3 + d^3. | 完全立方等式 (a^3 = b^3 + c^3 + d^3) | 求出所有滿足 a^3 = b^3 + c^3 + d^3 且 2 <= b <= c <= d <= a <= 200 的整數四元組。 | math, brute-force | [C](UVa-c/volume003/386_perfect-cubes.c) |
| UVa 389. Basically Speaking | Each input line contains a numeral, its source base, and its target base. Convert digits '0–9' and 'A–F' through their numeric value, then right-align the target representation in a field of width seven. Print 'ERROR' instead if the converted representation needs more than seven characters. | 基本進位制轉換 | 每行輸入一個數字字串、原進位與目標進位，進位範圍使用 '0'–'9'、'A'–'F' 表示數字。先依原進位解析數值，再除以目標進位取得輸出字串；結果在寬度 7 的欄位中靠右輸出，若轉換後超過 7 個字元則輸出 'ERROR'。 | math, base-conversion | [C](UVa-c/volume003/389_basically-speaking.c) |
| UVa 392. Polynomial Showdown | Format a degree 8 polynomial given its 9 integer coefficients, omitting zero terms and formatting signs cleanly. | 多項式美觀格式化輸出 | 給定 8 次多項式的 9 個係數，省略係數為 0 的項，美觀排版正負號與次方符號 (如 3x^2 - x + 5)。 | string, math | [C](UVa-c/volume003/392_polynomial-showdown.c) |
| UVa 394. Mapmaker | Compute memory address of multi-dimensional array elements given base address, element size, and dimension bounds. | 地圖製作者 (數組定址計算) | 給定多維數組的起始基底位址、元素大小及各維度上下界，計算指定下標的記憶體位址。 | data-structures, array-addressing | [C](UVa-c/volume003/394_mapmaker.c) |
| UVa 400. Unix ls | Format list of filenames into column-major order across 60-character terminal width like Unix ls command. | Unix 檔案列表多欄排版 | 模擬 Unix 'ls' 指令：將檔名依字典序排序，在 60 字元寬度限制下計算最多欄位數並以由上而下、由左至右排版。 | string, sorting, formatting | [C](UVa-c/volume004/400_unix-ls.c) |
| UVa 401. Palindromes | For every input string, independently test whether it reads the same backward and whether reversing it while replacing each character by its defined mirror yields the original. | UVa 406. Prime Cuts | List all primes <= N (counting 1 as a prime). Output 2C (or 2C-1) primes centered in the list. | 質數序列居中切割截取 | 列出小於等於 N 的質數 (本題將 1 視為質數)。若質數總數為偶數則從正中央截取 2C 個，為奇數則截取 2C-1 個。 | math, prime-sieve | [C](UVa-c/volume004/406_prime-cuts.c) |
| UVa 408. Uniform Generator | Determine if step size STEP and modulus MOD generate all integers 0..MOD-1 via seed = (seed + STEP) % MOD. | 均勻隨機產生器 (互質判定 GCD) | 判定線性同餘產生器 step 與 mod 能否生成 0 到 mod-1 的所有整數 (即 gcd(step, mod) == 1 則為 Good Choice)。 | math, number-theory, gcd | [C](UVa-c/volume004/408_uniform-generator.c) |

| UVa 410. Station Balance | Place S specimens into C centrifuge chambers (at most 2 specimens per chamber) to minimize imbalance relative to average weight. | 太空站重力配平 | 將 S 個樣本放入 C 個離心機室（每室最多 2 個樣本），求使各室重量與平均值之差絕對值總和最小的配平方案。 | greedy, sorting | [C](UVa-c/volume004/410_station-balance.c) |
| UVa 412. Pi | Given a set of integers, estimate pi using probability of randomly chosen pair being coprime (P = 6 / pi^2). | 由互質機率估算圓周率 Pi | 給定一組整數，計算任選兩數互質的機率 P，並利用蒙地卡羅數論公式 P = 6 / pi^2 估算圓周率 pi 的近似值。 | math, number-theory, probability | [C](UVa-c/volume004/412_pi.c) |

MPI Maelstrom | Find the minimum time to broadcast a message from processor 1 to all other processors in an MPI network (maximum shortest path). | MPI 訊息廣播全源最短傳遞時間 | 在處理器通訊網路中，求將廣播訊息自 1 號處理器傳送至其餘所有處理器所需的最短時間 (即單源最短路徑中的最大值)。 | graph, dijkstra, floyd-warshall | [C](UVa-c/volume004/423_mpi-maelstrom.c) |

Word Transformation | Find minimum transformations to change start word to target word, changing one letter at a time through words in the dictionary. | 單字變換最短梯路 BFS | 在單字庫中每次僅允許改變一個字母，求由起始單字變換至目標單字的最少變換步數 (單字梯 BFS 最短路)。 | graph, bfs, string | [C](UVa-c/volume004/429_word-transformation.c) |

Arbitrage (II) | Determine if an arbitrage opportunity exists in a currency exchange market (a sequence of trades that starts and ends at the same currency with net gain > 1). | 套匯 II (貨幣乘積正環Floyd-Warshall) | 判斷外匯市場中是否存在套匯套利機會（透過一系列貨幣兌換回到原貨幣後價值嚴格大於 1）。 | graph, floyd-warshall, shortest-path | [C](UVa-c/volume004/436_arbitrage-ii.c) |

Kibbles n Bits n Bits n Bits | Given hex expression 'A + B' or 'A - B', print 13-bit binary of A, operator, 13-bit binary of B, and decimal result. | 十六進位轉十三位元二進位運算 | 給定十六進位運算式 (如 '0x1A + 0x2')，格式化輸出 13 位元二進位、運算子與十進位計算結果。 | bit-manipulation, base-conversion, formatting | [C](UVa-c/volume004/446_kibbles-bits.c) |

Points in Figures: Rectangles, Circles, Triangles | Given a collection of rectangles, circles, and triangles, determine which figures contain a query 2D point (x, y). | 點在幾何圖形判定 (矩形、圓形、三角形) | 給定包含矩形、圓形與三角形的二維圖形集合，判定指定查詢點落於哪些圖形的內部。 | geometry | [C](UVa-c/volume004/478_points-in-figures-rectangles-circles-triangles.c) |

Rotate the complete collection 90 degrees clockwise and print it, so each output row is one original column read from the last input line upward. | 句子順時針旋轉 90 度輸出 | 將直到檔案結尾的每一行文字存成二維字元陣列，各列長度可不同，缺少的位置視為空白。以最長輸入列為輸出行數，對每個欄位從最後一列往第一列讀取，即得到順時針旋轉 90 度的結果；必要空白也要保留。 | string, 2d-array | [C](UVa-c/volume004/490_rotating-sentences.c) |

Use the symmetry 'C(n, k) = C(n, n-k)' and interleave multiplication with exact division so the factorials never need to be formed. Input ends with '0 0'. | 二項式係數精準計算 | 每筆輸入 'n'、'k'，要求精確輸出從 'n' 個元素取 'k' 個的組合數。先令 'k = min(k, n-k)'，再依序以 '答案 × (n-i+1) ÷ i' 計算，避免直接建立巨大階乘及不必要的中間值；讀到 '0 0' 時結束。 | math, combinatorics | [C](UVa-c/volume005/530_binomial-showdown.c) |

Parse the values, derive the missing quantity from 'P = U × I', and print it with two decimal places and its proper unit. | 物理功率、電壓、電流計算 | 每組輸入是一段文字，其中會出現 'P'（瓦特）、'U'（伏特）、'I'（安培）三項中的兩項，數值可能帶 'm'、'k'、'M' 倍率前綴。解析並換算成基本單位後，用 'P = U × I' 求第三項；按案例格式輸出未知量、兩位小數與正確單位。 | string, parsing, physics | [C](UVa-c/volume005/537_artificial-intelligence.c) |
| UVa 539. The Settlers of Catan | Find the length of the longest path in an undirected graph such that no edge is traversed more than once. | 卡坦島開拓者 (圖中最長無重複邊簡單路徑) | 在無向圖中尋找一條最長路徑，要求每條邊最多只能經過一次 (節點可重複造訪)，求最大路徑長度。 | graph, dfs, backtracking | [C](UVa-c/volume005/539_the-settlers-of-catan.c) |
| UVa 540. Team Queue | Simulate Team Queue: when an element arrives, it joins its team's sub-queue if teammates are already in queue. | 團隊佇列 (佇列之佇列模擬) | 模擬團隊佇列：新元素進入佇列時，若其所屬團隊已有人在佇列中，則插隊排在該團隊同伴身後；否則排在隊列最末端。 | data-structures, queue, simulation | [C](UVa-c/volume005/540_team-queue.c) |
| UVa 541. Error Correction | Given an 'N × N' binary matrix, verify that every row and column has an even sum. Print 'OK' if all parities are even, identify the one-based cell whose flip would fix the matrix when exactly one row and one column are odd, or print 'Corrupt' otherwise. Input ends with 'N = 0'. | 二維奇偶校驗矩陣修正 | 每組輸入 'N × N' 的 0/1 矩陣，檢查每一列與每一行的元素和是否皆為偶數。若全部通過就輸出 'OK'；若恰有一個奇數列與一個奇數行，翻轉兩者交點即可修復，輸出該一基座標；其他情況輸出 'Corrupt'。'N = 0' 結束。 | 2d-array, simulation | [C](UVa-c/volume005/541_error-correction.c) |
| UVa 542. France '98 | Given match winning probabilities between all pairs of 16 teams, compute each team's probability of winning the tournament. | 1998 世界盃淘汰賽奪冠機率 | 給定 16 支球隊兩兩對戰的勝率矩陣，計算每支球隊最終贏得世界盃冠軍的機率。 | probability, dynamic-programming, tournament | [C](UVa-c/volume005/542_france_98.c) |
| UVa 543. Goldbach's Conjecture | For each even integer 'n', find two odd primes 'a' and 'b' with 'n = a + b'. The required search starts with the smallest possible 'a', which produces the largest difference 'b - a'; print the decomposition, or the failure sentence if none is found. Input ends with zero. | 哥德巴赫猜想 | 每筆輸入一個偶數 'n'，尋找兩個奇質數 'a'、'b' 使 'n = a + b'。先用埃氏篩建立質數表，再由最小奇質數開始測試 'n-a'，因此找到的第一組同時使 'b-a' 最大；輸出等式，若找不到則輸出題目指定的失敗訊息，輸入 0 結束。 | math, number-theory, prime | [C](UVa-c/volume005/543_goldbachs-conjecture.c) |
| UVa 544. Heavy Cargo | Find path between city A and B that maximizes minimum bridge weight limit (bottleneck capacity). | 重載卡車貨運 (最大負載瓶頸路徑 Maximin) | 求兩城市間貨運卡車能通行的最大載重噸位 (即使得路徑上的最小承重橋樑噸位最大化，Maximin 問題)。 | graph, mst, dijkstra, shortest-path | [C](UVa-c/volume005/544_heavy-cargo.c) |
| UVa 548. Tree | Given inorder and postorder of weighted binary tree, find leaf node with minimum root-to-leaf path sum. | 中序與後序重構二元樹 (尋找最小權重路徑葉節點) | 給定二元樹的中序與後序遍歷，重構二元樹並找出從根結點到葉節點路徑權重和最小的葉節點數值。 | binary-tree, recursion, dfs | [C](UVa-c/volume005/548_tree.c) |
| UVa 555. Bridge Hands | Simulate bridge card dealing starting after dealer (N, E, S, W) and sort each player's 13 cards by suit (C, D, S, H) and rank (2..A). | 橋牌發牌模擬與手牌排序 | 模擬 52 張橋牌順時針發牌流程，並將四位玩家手牌按花色 (梅花 C < 方塊 D < 黑桃 S < 紅心 H) 與點數 (2..9, T, J, Q, K, A) 排序輸出。 | simulation, card-game, sorting | [C](UVa-c/volume005/555_bridge-hands.c) |
| UVa 556. Amazing | Simulate a robot traversing a maze following the right-hand wall rule until returning to start. | 走迷宮 (右手摸牆法則步數統計) | 模擬機器人以右手貼牆法則在迷宮中行走直到返回起點與初始朝向，統計各空格被拜訪的次數分佈。 | simulation, maze, grid | [C](UVa-c/volume005/556_amazing.c) |
| UVa 558. Wormholes | Determine if directed graph with negative edge weights contains a negative-weight cycle (time travel). | 蟲洞時空倒流 (負權迴路 Bellman-Ford 檢測) | 在含有負權邊的有向圖中，判定是否存在負權迴路使得旅行者能回到過去 (Bellman-Ford 演算法)。 | graph, bellman-ford, negative-cycle | [C](UVa-c/volume005/558_wormholes.c) |
| UVa 562. Dividing coins | Divide m coins between two people such that the difference between the two totals is minimized. | 分金幣 (0/1 背包子集和最小差劃分) | 將 m 枚不同面額的金幣分給兩人，求兩人所分得金幣總價值差額的最小值 (0/1 背包容量為 Sum/2)。 | dynamic-programming, knapsack | [C](UVa-c/volume005/562_dividing-coins.c) |
| UVa 567. Risk | Given board graph of 20 countries, find minimum armies needed to move between country pairs (BFS shortest path). | 戰國風雲 (BFS 最短路徑走訪) | 在 20 個國家的世界地圖圖形中，求從出發國至目的國所需經過的最少邊數 (BFS 最短路徑)。 | graph, bfs, shortest-path | [C](UVa-c/volume005/567_risk.c) |
| UVa 568. Just the Facts | For each 'N', determine the rightmost nonzero decimal digit of 'N!'. During repeated multiplication, remove trailing zeroes and retain only enough low digits to keep the value bounded, then print 'N' and the digit in the required aligned format. | 階乘末尾最後一個非零數字 | 每行輸入 'N'，要求 'N!' 的十進位表示中最右邊非 0 的數字。逐次乘入 1 到 'N'，每步把末尾 0 除去並保留適量低位以避免數值膨脹；最後取個位數，按題目規定對齊輸出 'N -> 答案'。 | math, number-theory | [C](UVa-c/volume005/568_just-the-facts.c) |
| UVa 571. Jugs | Given two jugs of capacity Ca and Cb and target N, output a sequence of fill, empty, and pour operations to measure N liters in jug B. | 倒水問題 | 給定容量為 Ca 與 Cb 的水壺及目標水量 N，求裝滿、倒空、互倒等操作序列，使 B 壺內恰有 N 升水。 | bfs, state-machine, simulation | [C](UVa-c/volume005/571_jugs.c) |
| UVa 572. Oil Deposits | A grid contains oil cells marked '@' and empty cells marked '*'. Cells belong to the same deposit when they connect horizontally, vertically, or diagonally. Count and print the number of eight-direction connected components for each grid; '0 0' ends the input. | 油田連通塊數量 | 每組輸入網格列數、行數及由 '@'、'*' 組成的地圖。兩個油藏格 '@' 若在水平、垂直或斜向八個方向相鄰就屬於同一油田；每遇到尚未造訪的 '@' 就以 DFS 標記整個連通塊並將答案加一。逐組輸出油田數，'0 0' 結束。 | graph, dfs, flood-fill | [C](UVa-c/volume005/572_oil-deposits.c) |
| UVa 573. The Snail | A snail climbs 'U' feet during the first day, loses 'F' percent of that original climbing distance from its daily ability after each day, and slides 'D' feet each night in a well of height 'H'. Simulate the day and night events in order and report the first day of success above the well or failure below zero. A zero height terminates… | 蝸牛爬井 | 每筆輸入井高 'H'、首日爬升 'U'、每夜滑落 'D' 與疲勞百分比 'F'。每天先以當日能力向上爬並立即檢查是否超過井口；若未成功，再向下滑並檢查是否低於 0，之後把次日爬升能力減少首日能力的 'F%'，最低為 0。輸出首次成功或失敗的天數，'H = 0' 結束。 | simulation | [C](UVa-c/volume005/573_the-snail.c) |
| UVa 574. Sum It Up | Find all unique combinations of a multiset of integers that sum up to target t, outputting in non-increasing order. | 子集總和全部組合列舉 (剪枝與去重回溯) | 給定目標和 t 與整數多重集，回溯列舉出所有總和恰為 t 的不重複子集，並按降序排列輸出。 | backtracking, recursion, subset-sum | [C](UVa-c/volume005/574_sum-it-up.c) |
| UVa 575. Skew Binary | Convert each skew-binary numeral to decimal. Counting positions from one at the right, a digit at position 'k' has weight '2^k - 1'; sum every digit times its weight and print the result. The input string '0' terminates processing. | 斜二進位制轉十進位 | 每行是一個斜二進位字串，從最右位開始編號 1，第 'k' 位權重為 '2^k - 1'。將每位數字值乘上對應權重後相加，即得十進位答案並逐行輸出；當整行只有 '0' 時結束。 | math, base-conversion | [C](UVa-c/volume005/575_skew-binary.c) |
| UVa 576. Haiku Review | Determine if a 3-phrase Haiku poem separated by '/' conforms to the 5-7-5 syllable structure, or report the index of the first incorrect phrase. | 日本俳句 5-7-5 音節規則審查 | 審查以斜線 '/' 分隔的三段式日本俳句是否符合 5-7-5 音節規範；若不符合則輸出首個出錯之段落編號 (1, 2, 3)；若正確輸出 'Y'。 | string | [C](UVa-c/volume005/576_haiku-review.c) |
| UVa 579. Clock Hands | For each time 'H:M', compute the smaller angle between the analog clock's hour and minute hands. The hour hand advances continuously as minutes pass, so compare '30H + 0.5M' with '6M', fold angles above 180 degrees, and print three decimal places. Input ends with '0:00'. | 時鐘時針與分針夾角 | 每筆輸入格式為 'H:M' 的時間。分針角度為 '6M'，時針除了每小時 30 度外還會隨分鐘前進，因此角度為 '30H + 0.5M'；兩者差的絕對值若超過 180 度就以 360 減去它。輸出較小夾角至小數點後三位，'0:00' 結束。 | math, geometry | [C](UVa-c/volume005/579_clock-hands.c) |
| UVa 580. Critical Mass | Find the number of stacks of n uranium (U) and lead (L) cubes that contain at least 3 consecutive uranium cubes (dangerous). | 臨界質量 (禁忌模式計數DP) | 求長度為 n 由鈾 (U) 與鉛 (L) 方塊構成的堆疊中，包含至少 3 個連續鈾方塊的排列數。 | dp, combinatorics | [C](UVa-c/volume005/580_critical-mass.c) |
| UVa 583. Prime Factors | Factor each nonzero signed integer into prime factors and print an equality joined by 'x' symbols. Input ends with zero. | 整數質因數分解輸出 | 每行輸入一個非 0 整數 'N'，輸出 'N = f1 x f2 x ...' 的完整質因數分解，相同質因數須依次重複列出。若 'N' 為負數，先輸出因數 '-1'，再分解其絕對值；以從小到大的試除法取出因數，最後剩餘大於 1 的值亦為質數。輸入 0 結束。 | math, number-theory, prime | [C](UVa-c/volume005/583_prime-factors.c) |
| UVa 584. Bowling | Calculate total bowling score from a roll sequence with strikes ('X'), spares ('/'), and numeric pinfall counts. | 保齡球計分系統模擬 | 依據標準保齡球 10 格規則計算全中 ('X')、補中 ('/') 與一般擲球的總得分。 | simulation, rule-based | [C](UVa-c/volume005/584_bowling.c) |
| UVa 587. There's treasure everywhere! | Follow navigation directions (N, NE, E, SE, S, SW, W, NW) with steps to find final (x, y) coordinates and Euclidean distance. | 到處都是寶藏！(二維羅盤方位向量累加) | 依羅盤 8 方位步數 (正向為 step，斜向各分量為 step / sqrt(2)) 累計二維位移向量，求寶藏最終座標與直線距離。 | geometry, math, parsing | [C](UVa-c/volume005/587_theres-treasure-everywhere.c) |
| UVa 591. Box of Bricks | Given the heights of several stacks containing a divisible total number of bricks, move individual bricks until every stack has the same height. | UVa 594. One Little, Two Little, Three Little Endians | Convert 32-bit signed integer between little-endian and big-endian byte orders. | 大端序與小端序 32位元整數轉換 | 將 32 位元有號整數的 4 個 Byte 順序反轉 (在小端序與大端序之間相互轉換)。 | bit-manipulation | [C](UVa-c/volume005/594_one-little-two-little-three-little-endians.c) |

The Forrest for the Trees | Given edge list and isolated nodes, count how many trees (size >= 2) and how many acorns (isolated vertices) exist. | 樹與橡果 (並查集連通分支與孤立點計數) | 給定無環森林的邊與頂點列表，計算其中由 2 個以上節點構成的「樹木」數量與單獨 1 個節點的「橡果」數量。 | graph, dsu, tree | [C](UVa-c/volume005/599_the-forrest-for-the-trees.c) |

Counterfeit Dollar | Given 3 balance scale weighings of 12 coins, deduce which coin is counterfeit and whether it is heavy or light. | 假幣辨識 (天平三次稱重邏輯推理) | 12 枚銀幣中恰有一枚假幣 (重量較重或較輕)。給定天平 3 次秤重記錄，推理出哪一枚是假幣且偏重或偏輕。 | logic, simulation, brute-force | [C](UVa-c/volume006/608_counterfeit-dollar.c) |

Morning division leaves 0 for monkey. | 椰子難題 (整數整除模擬) | N 個人與 1 隻猴子分椰子：每人各自將椰子等分 N 堆給猴子 1 顆並藏起 1 堆，隔天早晨恰好能等分給 N 人（猴子分得 0 顆），求最大人數 N。 | math, simulation | [C](UVa-c/volume006/616_coconuts-revisited.c) |
| UVa 621. Secret Research | Each decimal-looking result string belongs to one of four experiment classes. Print '+' for the exact values '1', '4', or '78'; '-' for strings ending in '35'; '*' for strings beginning with '9' and ending in '4'; and '?' for strings beginning with '190'. | 秘密實驗結果加密辨識 | 輸入先給測試筆數，再逐行給一個結果字串。若字串恰為 '1'、'4'、'78'，輸出 '+'；若末兩字元是 '35'，輸出 '-'；若首字元為 '9' 且末字元為 '4'，輸出 '*'；若以 '190' 開頭則輸出 '?'。每筆只輸出一個分類符號。 | string | [C](UVa-c/volume006/621_secret-research.c) |
| UVa 623. 500! | For each input 'N', print the exact decimal value of 'N!' in the format 'N!' followed by the number on the next line. | UVa 624. CD | Given tape length N and track durations, select subset of tracks to maximize total time without exceeding N. | 錄音帶容量最大化 (0/1 背包與曲目路徑還原) | 在給定錄音帶容量 N 的限制下，挑選曲目使得總播放時間最接近 N 且不超額，並輸出所挑選的曲目清單。 | dynamic-programming, knapsack, backtracking | [C](UVa-c/volume006/624_cd.c) |
| UVa 627. The Net | Find the shortest routing path between routers in an unweighted communication network. | 網路最短路徑路由 | 在無權路由器通訊網路中尋找兩節點之間的最短路徑並輸出路徑節點序列。 | graph, bfs, shortest-path | [C](UVa-c/volume006/627_the-net.c) |
| UVa 628. Passwords | Given a dictionary of words and password rules containing '#' (word from dict) and '0' (digit 0-9), generate all valid passwords. | 密碼字典與規則生成回溯搜尋 | 給定單詞字典與密碼規則字串 (包含 '#' 代表替換字典單詞，'0' 代表替換數字 0-9)，窮舉生成所有可能密碼。 | backtracking, string | [C](UVa-c/volume006/628_passwords.c) |
| UVa 630. Anagrams (II) | Given a vocabulary list, find and list all vocabulary words that are anagrams of a given query word. | 字彙變位詞檢索 | 給定字彙字典，對於每次查詢單字，輸出字典中所有由相同字母組成的變位詞（保持字典中的出現順序）。 | string, sorting, hash-table | [C](UVa-c/volume006/630_anagrams_ii.c) |

| UVa 637. Booklet Printing | Simulate folding sheets of paper into a booklet: determine page numbers printed on front and back of each sheet for an N-page document. | 小冊子雙面列印頁碼排列模擬 | 模擬將紙張對折裝訂成小冊子：計算包含 N 頁的文件在每張紙正面與背面左右兩側應列印的頁碼 (不足處補 Blank)。 | simulation | [C](UVa-c/volume006/637_booklet-printing.c) |
| UVa 639. Don't Get Rooked | Place maximum number of rooks on n x n board with walls 'X' such that no two rooks can attack each other. | 不要被城堡吃掉 (帶障礙牆之城堡最大放置) | 在 n x n (n <= 4) 的棋盤上放置互不攻擊的城堡。牆壁 'X' 會阻隔城堡的攻擊視線，求最大擺放數量。 | backtracking, brute-force | [C](UVa-c/volume006/639_dont-get-hooked.c) |

Determine and print the winner for each input target under optimal play. | 乘法博弈遊戲 | 每筆輸入一個目標值 'n'，遊戲從 'p = 1' 開始，由 Stan 先手，兩人輪流選擇 2 到 9 的整數乘上目前的 'p'；誰先使 'p ≥ n' 就獲勝。利用雙方最佳策略的勝負區間，依序乘 9（Stan 能強制達成的上界）與 2（Ollie 能回應的上界），判斷並輸出 'Stan wins.' 或 'Ollie wins.'。 | math, game-theory | [C](UVa-c/volume008/847_a-multiplication-game.c) |

| UVa 1339. Ancient Cipher | Determine if string A can be transformed into string B by a combination of letter substitution bijection and character permutation. | 古代密碼字母置換與排列判定 | 判定字串 A 是否能透過字母單射置換與字母順序重排轉換為字串 B (字元各自出現頻率集合相同)。 | string, sorting | [C](UVa-c/volume013/1339_ancient-cipher.c) |
| UVa 1368. DNA Consensus String | Each test case contains 'm' DNA strings of equal length 'n'. Construct a consensus string that minimizes the sum of Hamming distances to all input strings by choosing a most frequent nucleotide in every column; resolve frequency ties in lexicographic order 'A', 'C', 'G', 'T'. Print the consensus followed by the minimum total distance. | DNA 共識字串 | 每組輸入 'm' 條等長為 'n' 的 DNA 序列。對每個字元位置分別統計 'A'、'C'、'G'、'T' 次數，選擇出現最多的鹼基可使該欄與所有序列不同的數量最少；若同頻則依 'A < C < G < T' 取字典序最小者。輸出組成的共識字串，以及它和所有輸入序列的漢明距離總和。 | string, greedy | [C](UVa-c/volume013/1368_dna-consensus-string.c) |
| UVa 1395. Slim Spanning Tree | Find a spanning tree that minimizes the difference between the maximum and minimum edge weights. | 苗條生成樹 (最小化極差) | 在給定的無向連通圖中，求一棵生成樹使得最大邊權與最小邊權之差最小。 | graph, mst, kruskal, two-pointers | [C](UVa-c/volume013/1395_slim_spanning_tree.c) |
| UVa 1583. Digit Generator | Find the smallest generator M such that M + sum_of_digits(M) = N, or 0 if no generator exists. | 最小生成元數值逆向查找 | 若整數 M 加上其各數位之和等於 N，則稱 M 為 N 的生成元。求 N 的最小生成元，若不存在則回傳 0。 | math | [C](UVa-c/volume015/1583_digit-generator.c) |
| UVa 1584. Circular Sequence | Given a circular DNA sequence of length N, find its lexicographically smallest representation among all N cyclic rotations. | 環狀 DNA 序列最小字典序表示法 | 環狀 DNA 序列由 A, C, G, T 組成，長度為 N，求其在所有 N 種循環旋轉切點下的最小字典序字串。 | string | [C](UVa-c/volume015/1584_circular-sequence.c) |
| UVa 1585. Score | Each test case is an 'O' and 'X' result string. An 'O' scores one more point than the immediately preceding consecutive 'O', starting at one, while an 'X' resets the streak to zero. Sum the points and print the total score for each string. | OX 答題連續得分計算 | 輸入先給測試筆數，每筆是一串 'O' 與 'X'。連續答對時，第 1 個 'O' 得 1 分、第 2 個得 2 分，依此遞增；遇到 'X' 就把連續次數歸零，下一個 'O' 重新從 1 分開始。線性掃描並累加每次得分，逐組輸出總分。 | string, simulation | [C](UVa-c/volume015/1585_score.c) |
| UVa 1586. Molar mass | Compute molar mass of chemical formula consisting of C (12.01), H (1.008), O (16.00), and N (14.01) with optional multi-digit integer counts. | 化學分子量莫耳質量計算 | 給定僅含 C (12.01)、H (1.008)、O (16.00) 與 N (14.01) 的化學分子式，計算其莫耳質量 (分子量)。 | string, parsing | [C](UVa-c/volume015/1586_molar-mass.c) |
| UVa 1587. Box | Given 6 rectangular pallets with dimensions (w, h), determine if they can form a rectangular cuboid. | 盒子 (長方體面匹配驗證) | 給定 6 塊長方形木板的長寬尺寸，判定它們是否能拼成一個三維長方體。 | geometry, sorting | [C](UVa-c/volume015/1587_box.c) |
| UVa 1588. Kickdown | Two tooth strips of heights 1 and 2 must fit into a container of height 3 without teeth overlapping (tooth heights at matching positions sum to <= 3). Find min length. | 齒條嚙合最短總長度 | 兩根齒高為 1 與 2 的齒條需放入高度為 3 的槽中，任何重疊位置齒高之和不可超過 3，求最小包絡長度。 | string, simulation | [C](UVa-c/volume015/1588_kickdown.c) |
| UVa 1594. Ducci Sequence | Repeatedly transform n-tuple (a1..an) into (\|a1-a2\|, \|a2-a3\|, ..., \|an-a1\|). Determine if it reaches all zeros (ZERO) or enters a cycle (LOOP). | 杜奇序列 (相鄰差循環收斂) | 反覆將 n 元組轉換為相鄰項差值絕對值數組，判斷是在 1000 步內變為全 0 (ZERO) 還是進入非零循環 (LOOP)。 | simulation, array | [C](UVa-c/volume015/1594_ducci-sequence.c) |
| UVa 1644. Prime Gap | Given composite number k, find length of prime gap containing k (difference between smallest prime > k and largest prime < k). If k is prime, gap is 0. | 質數間隙長度 | 給定合數 k，求包含 k 的質數間隙長度（即大於 k 的最小質數減去小於 k 的最大質數；若 k 本身為質數則間隙為 0）。 | math, prime, sieve | [C](UVa-c/volume016/1644_prime-gap.c) |
| UVa 10000. Longest Paths | Given a directed graph and a starting vertex, determine the greatest number of edges on a path beginning there. If several longest paths end at different vertices, report the smallest ending vertex number together with the path length. | 最長路徑 | 輸入一張有向圖及指定起點；任務是找出由起點出發可走到的最長路徑。程式以記憶化 DFS 計算每個頂點往後的最佳長度與終點，長度相同時選編號較小的終點，最後輸出最長距離及終點。 | graph, dag, dynamic-programming, longest-path | [C](UVa-c/volume100/10000_longest-paths.c) |
| UVa 10001. Garden of Eden | Determine if a circular 1D cellular automaton configuration is reachable from some ancestor state, or if it is an unreachable 'Garden of Eden' state. | 伊甸園 (一維細胞自動機前驅狀態判定) | 判斷一維循環細胞自動機的當前狀態是否具備前驅狀態（若無前驅狀態則為「伊甸園狀態」）。 | backtracking, cellular-automata | [C](UVa-c/volume100/10001_garden-of-eden.c) |
| UVa 10002. Center of Masses | Find the center of mass (centroid) of a uniform planar convex polygon. | 凸多邊形質心計算 | 求均勻凸多邊形平面薄片的質心（重心）坐標。 | geometry, centroid, polygon-area | [C](UVa-c/volume100/10002_center_of_masses.c) |
| UVa 10003. Cutting Sticks | A stick of a given length must be cut at specified positions. Each cut costs the length of the current segment being cut, so the goal is to choose the cutting order that minimizes the total cost. | 切割木棍 | 輸入木棍總長與所有必須切割的位置；同一組切點因執行順序不同，切割當下的區段長度也不同。程式以兩切點間的區間作為狀態，枚舉最後或第一個切點並合併左右成本，輸出完成全部切割的最小總成本。 | dynamic-programming, interval-dp | [C](UVa-c/volume100/10003_cutting-sticks.c) |
| UVa 10004. Bicoloring | Given an undirected graph, decide whether its vertices can be colored with two colors so that every edge joins vertices of different colors. The result states whether the graph is bicolorable. | 雙色著色 | 輸入無向圖的頂點與邊；任務是判斷能否只用兩種顏色，使每條邊的兩端顏色都不同。程式從起點做 BFS，替未著色鄰點配置相反顏色；若遇到同色相鄰頂點便判定失敗，最後輸出是否可雙色著色。 | graph, bfs, bipartite | [C](UVa-c/volume100/10004_bicoloring.c) |
| UVa 10006. Carmichael Numbers | For each input integer, determine whether it is a composite Carmichael number, meaning that it satisfies a^n congruent to a modulo n for every integer a between 2 and n-1. | 卡邁克爾數 | 輸入若干整數；題目要求判斷每個數是否為卡邁克爾數。程式先排除質數，再對候選底數使用快速模冪驗證費馬型同餘式；全部成立才輸出卡邁克爾數的判定，否則視為一般數。 | math, number-theory, modular-exponentiation | [C](UVa-c/volume100/10006_carmichael-numbers.c) |
| UVa 10007. Count the Trees | Find the number of completely labeled binary search trees with n distinct keys: n! * C_n = (2n)! / (n + 1)!. | 計算二元樹數量 | 求含 n 個不同節點的標號二元樹總數，計算公式為 n! * 卡特蘭數 C_n = (2n)! / (n+1)!。 | math, catalan-numbers, big-integer | [C](UVa-c/volume100/10007_count-the-trees.c) |
| UVa 10008. What's Cryptanalysis? | Read several lines of text, count letters without regard to case, and print every letter that appears. Letters are ordered by decreasing frequency and then alphabetically when counts tie. | 什麼是密碼分析？ | 輸入多行英文文字；忽略大小寫後統計 A 到 Z 各字母出現次數。輸出時先按次數由多至少排列，次數相同則按字母順序排列，且只列出實際出現過的字母。 | string, frequency-counting, sorting | [C](UVa-c/volume100/10008_whats-cryptanalysis.c) |
| UVa 10009. All Roads Lead Where? | Given roads that form a tree and pairs of cities, print the unique route between each requested pair. City initials are concatenated in the order in which the route visits them. | 條條大路通何方？ | 輸入構成樹狀結構的城市道路，以及多組起訖城市查詢；因圖為樹，每組城市間只有一條簡單路徑。程式分別向父節點回溯以找出共同祖先，再串接上行與下行路徑，輸出依序經過的城市代號。 | tree, lca, graph, bfs | [C](UVa-c/volume100/10009_all-roads-lead-where.c) |
| UVa 10010. Where's Waldorf? | Search a letter grid for each query word in any of the eight straight directions, ignoring letter case. Report the row and column of the first character of the earliest matching occurrence. | 沃爾多在哪裡？ | 輸入一個字母方格與多個查詢單字；每個單字可沿水平、垂直或斜向共八個方向出現，配對時不分大小寫。程式依列、欄順序嘗試每個起點與方向，輸出最先找到之單字首字母的一基底座標。 | string, 2d-array, search | [C](UVa-c/volume100/10010_wheres-waldorf.c) |
| UVa 10012. How Big Is It? | Arrange circles of given radii inside a horizontal rectangular container so that they rest without overlapping. Find the ordering and placements that minimize the required container width. | 到底有多大？ | 輸入多個圓的半徑；任務是決定圓的排列順序與水平位置，使圓彼此不重疊且包住所有圓的矩形寬度最小。完整作法會以切圓幾何計算合法中心距離，再枚舉不同排列取最小寬度 | geometry, backtracking, math | [C](UVa-c/volume100/10012_how-big-is-it.c) |
| UVa 10013. Super Long Sums | Two extremely long nonnegative integers are supplied as pairs of corresponding decimal digits. Add them exactly and print the complete decimal sum. | 超長整數相加 | 輸入兩個長度很大的非負整數，各位數以成對數字由最高位到最低位給出。程式先保存每一位的和，再從最低位向左處理進位，最後輸出不能用一般整數型別直接容納的完整十進位結果。 | math, big-integer, simulation | [C](UVa-c/volume100/10013_super-long-sums.c) |
| UVa 10014. Simple Calculations | A sequence satisfies a second-order relation involving known constants c_i, while its two boundary values are given. Compute and print the first unknown term a_1. | 簡單計算 | 輸入序列長度、兩端值 a_0 與 a_{n+1}，以及各項常數 c_i；序列須滿足題目給定的二階關係式。程式將所有等式加權整理成 a_1 的封閉公式，計算後依題目格式輸出第一個未知項。 | math, linear-algebra | [C](UVa-c/volume100/10014_simple-calculations.c) |
| UVa 10015. Joseph's Cousin | People stand in a circle and are removed one by one, using successive prime numbers as the step sizes. For each population size, determine the position of the final survivor. | 約瑟夫的表親 | 輸入圓圈中的人數；淘汰過程依序使用第 1、2、3 個質數等作為每輪步長。程式先產生所需質數，再以約瑟夫問題的索引遞推或模擬更新存活位置，輸出最後留下者的編號。 | math, simulation, josephus | [C](UVa-c/volume100/10015_josephs-cousin.c) |
| UVa 10018. Reverse and Add | Starting with an integer, repeatedly reverse its decimal digits and add the reversed value to the current number. Report how many iterations are needed to obtain a palindrome and the resulting palindrome. | 反轉再相加 | 輸入整數後，反轉其十進位數字並與原數相加，再對新數重複相同步驟。程式每輪檢查結果是否為回文，最後輸出迭代次數與首次得到的回文數。 | math, simulation, palindrome | [C](UVa-c/volume100/10018_reverse-and-add.c) |
| UVa 10019. Funny Encryption Method | For each decimal integer N, count the one bits in N's ordinary binary representation and in the binary value obtained when N's decimal digits are interpreted as hexadecimal digits. Print both counts. | 有趣的加密方法 | 輸入十進位整數 N；第一個答案是 N 直接轉為二進位後的 1 位元數，第二個答案則先把 N 的十進位字串當作十六進位表示，再統計該值的 1 位元數。程式以位元消去法計數並輸出兩個結果。 | math, bit-manipulation, base-conversion | [C](UVa-c/volume100/10019_funny-encryption-method.c) |
| UVa 10020. Minimal Coverage | From a collection of intervals, choose the smallest subset whose union completely covers [0, M]. Print the selected intervals, or report that no complete cover exists. | 最少區間覆蓋 | 輸入目標區間 [0, M] 與一批候選線段；任務是使用最少線段完整覆蓋目標。程式先依左端點排序，在目前已覆蓋位置之前可起始的線段中，每次選右端延伸最遠者；若中途無法前進便輸出無解，否則輸出數量與選中的線段。 | greedy, sorting | [C](UVa-c/volume100/10020_minimal-coverage.c) |
| UVa 10023. Square Root | Given a nonnegative integer too large for built-in numeric types, compute its exact integer square root and print the decimal result. | 平方根 | 輸入位數很長的非負整數；任務是求其精確整數平方根，不能依賴一般固定寬度型別。完整作法可用十進位大整數搭配直式開平方逐段決定答案數字 | math, big-integer | [C](UVa-c/volume100/10023_square-root.c) |
| UVa 10025. The 1 2 ... n = k Problem | Find the smallest n for which signs can be assigned to 1, 2, ..., n so that their signed sum equals a given integer k. | 1、2、…、n 等於 k 問題 | 輸入整數 k；要找最小 n，使 1 到 n 的每個數前可選正號或負號，且總和恰為 k。程式取 \|k\|，逐步累加三角形數，直到總和不小於 k 且兩者差為偶數，輸出此時的最小 n。 | math, greedy | [C](UVa-c/volume100/10025_the-1-2--n-%3D-k-problem.c) |
| UVa 10026. Shoemaker's Problem | Each job has a processing time and a fine charged for every day it remains unfinished. Order all jobs to minimize the total fine, breaking equivalent choices by original job number. | 鞋匠問題 | 輸入多項工作，每項包含完成所需天數與每日延誤罰金。任務是安排工作順序，使累積罰金最小；程式以交叉乘積比較工作時間與罰金比率，避免浮點誤差，若優先順序相同則依原編號，最後輸出工作次序。 | greedy, sorting | [C](UVa-c/volume100/10026_shoemakers-problem.c) |
| UVa 10029. Edit Step Ladders | From a lexicographically ordered dictionary, find the longest ladder in which every next word is later in dictionary order and differs from its predecessor by one insertion, deletion, or replacement. | 編輯階梯 | 輸入依字典序排列的單字；若後一個單字可由前一個單字經一次插入、刪除或替換得到，就能接在階梯後。完整作法會對每個單字查找所有可能的先驅並做動態規劃，輸出可形成的最長階梯長度 | dynamic-programming, string | [C](UVa-c/volume100/10029_edit-step-ladders.c) |
| UVa 10032. Tug of War | Split people into two teams whose sizes differ by at most one and whose total weights are as close as possible. Print the two team weights in increasing order. | 拔河 | 輸入每位成員的體重；必須分成兩隊，人數相同或只差一人，並讓總體重差最小。程式以重量為索引、以位元表示可用人數的 0/1 背包狀態，從總重一半向下找符合隊伍人數的最佳重量，輸出兩隊總重。 | dynamic-programming, knapsack, bit-manipulation | [C](UVa-c/volume100/10032_tug-of-war.c) |
| UVa 10033. Interpreter | Execute a program for a small decimal virtual machine with ten registers and one thousand memory cells. Stop on instruction 100 and report the number of instructions executed, including the halt. | 直譯器 | 輸入一串三位數機器指令並載入記憶體；各百位數操作碼會設定、加乘、載入、儲存或條件跳躍，所有暫存器運算依規則取模。程式逐步取指、解碼與執行，遇到 100 停機，輸出執行過的指令總數。 | simulation | [C](UVa-c/volume100/10033_interpreter.c) |
| UVa 10034. Freckles | Given points in the plane, connect all of them with line segments using the minimum possible total length. Print the weight of the Euclidean minimum spanning tree. | 雀斑 | 輸入平面上各點座標；任務是用線段把所有點連成同一連通網路，並使墨線總長最小。程式把任兩點的歐幾里得距離視為邊權，以 Prim 演算法建立最小生成樹，輸出其總長度。 | graph, mst, kruskal, prim | [C](UVa-c/volume100/10034_freckles.c) |
| UVa 10035. Primary Arithmetic | For each pair of nonnegative decimal integers, count how many carry operations occur in ordinary right-to-left addition. Print a grammatically appropriate message for zero, one, or multiple carries. | 基礎算術 | 輸入多組非負整數，直到終止資料；任務是計算直式加法中發生幾次進位。程式由個位數起逐位相加並帶入上一位進位，累計和達到十的次數，再依 0、1 或多次輸出相應訊息。 | math, simulation, elementary-math | [C](UVa-c/volume100/10035_primary-arithmetic.c) |
| UVa 10036. Divisibility | Insert either a plus or a minus sign before each number in a sequence and decide whether some resulting signed sum is divisible by K. | 整除性 | 輸入整數序列與除數 K；每加入一個數時可選擇加或減，題目要求判斷是否存在使最終總和可被 K 整除的配置。程式只保留各階段可達的模 K 餘數，轉移到加、減兩種新餘數，最後輸出可整除或不可整除。 | dynamic-programming, math | [C](UVa-c/volume100/10036_divisibility.c) |
| UVa 10037. Bridge | People with different crossing times must all cross a bridge at night using one flashlight, with at most two people crossing together. Find the minimum total time and a corresponding crossing plan. | 過橋 | 輸入每個人的過橋時間；每次最多兩人同行且手電筒必須被帶回，同行耗時取較慢者。程式先排序，反覆比較「最快者分別護送」與「兩位最快者接力」兩種搬運最慢兩人的策略，得到並輸出最小總時間與過橋步驟。 | greedy, math | [C](UVa-c/volume100/10037_bridge.c) |
| UVa 10038. Jolly Jumpers | A sequence of length n is jolly when the absolute differences of consecutive values contain every integer from 1 through n-1 exactly once. Classify each input sequence. | 快樂跳躍者 | 每筆輸入先給序列長度 n 與 n 個整數；計算相鄰元素差的絕對值後，若 1 到 n-1 全部恰好出現一次，便是 Jolly。程式用布林陣列記錄差值，遇到越界或重複即可判定失敗，最後輸出分類結果。 | array, simulation | [C](UVa-c/volume100/10038_jolly-jumpers.c) |
| UVa 10041. Vito's Family | Given the street numbers where Vito's relatives live, choose a street for Vito that minimizes the sum of walking distances to all relatives. Print that minimum total distance. | 維托的家族 | 輸入親戚住所的街道號碼；維托要選一個住址，使到所有親戚家的距離總和最小。程式將街道號碼排序並取中位數作為最佳位置，再加總各住所與中位數的絕對差，輸出最小總距離。 | math, sorting, median | [C](UVa-c/volume100/10041_vitos-family.c) |
| UVa 10042. Smith Numbers | A Smith number is composite and has a digit sum equal to the sum of the digits in its prime factorization, counting repeated factors. For each input, find the smallest Smith number larger than it. | 史密斯數 | 輸入整數 n；要從 n+1 起尋找最小史密斯數。程式排除質數，對候選數做質因數分解並保留重複因子，比較候選數本身的數位和與所有質因數的數位和；相等時輸出該候選數。 | math, number-theory, prime | [C](UVa-c/volume100/10042_smith-numbers.c) |
| UVa 10044. Erdos Numbers | Build a coauthorship graph from paper author lists. For every queried author, print the length of the shortest collaboration chain from Erdos, or infinity when no such chain exists. | 厄多斯數 | 輸入多篇論文的作者名單與查詢作者；同一篇論文的作者彼此建立合作邊。完整作法以 Erdos 為起點做 BFS，得到每位作者的最短合作層數；查詢時輸出作者名稱與厄多斯數，無法連通則輸出 infinity。 | graph, bfs, string | [C](UVa-c/volume100/10044_erdos-numbers.c) |
| UVa 10047. The Monocycle | Move a monocycle through a blocked grid. It may turn or move forward, its wheel color advances after each forward move, and it must reach the target showing green; find the minimum time or report impossibility. | 獨輪車 | 輸入含障礙、起點與終點的網格；獨輪車每秒可左轉、右轉或前進，前進時輪面顏色會在五色間循環。完整作法把位置、朝向與顏色一起當成 BFS 狀態，尋找抵達終點且顯示綠色的最短時間 | graph, bfs, shortest-path | [C](UVa-c/volume100/10047_the-monocycle.c) |
| UVa 10048. Audiophobia | Roads have sound levels. For each pair of cities, find a route whose loudest road is as quiet as possible, and print that minimum possible maximum level or state that no path exists. | 恐聲症 | 輸入城市、道路及每條道路的噪音值，再給多組起訖查詢；路徑代價定義為途中最大邊權，目標是讓此最大值最小。程式以 Floyd–Warshall 的 minimax 轉移預先計算所有城市對，查詢時輸出最佳噪音值或 no path。 | graph, shortest-path, floyd-warshall | [C](UVa-c/volume100/10048_audiophobia.c) |
| UVa 10049. Self-describing Sequence | Compute the n-th value in Golomb's self-describing sequence where f(n) is the number of times n appears in the sequence. | 自描述序列 (Golomb 序列) | 計算 Golomb 自描述序列中的第 n 項數值 f(n)（f(n) 為 n 在序列中出現的次數）。 | math, binary-search, dp | [C](UVa-c/volume100/10049_self-describing-sequence.c) |
| UVa 10050. Hartals | Political parties call strikes at fixed day intervals during a period of N days. Count lost working days, excluding Fridays and Saturdays and counting overlapping strikes only once. | 罷工 | 輸入模擬天數與各政黨的罷工週期；每逢週期倍數便罷工，但週五、週六本來就不是工作日，不能列入損失。程式標記所有有效罷工日，重疊日期只算一次，最後輸出損失的工作天數。 | simulation, array | [C](UVa-c/volume100/10050_hartals.c) |
| UVa 10051. Tower of Cubes | Stack cubes of decreasing index such that the bottom face of the upper cube has the same color as the top face of the lower cube. Maximize height. | 立方體堆高塔 (最大加權LIS/DAG最長路) | 依索引遞減的順序疊放立方體，使得上層方塊底面顏色等於下層方塊頂面顏色，求最大堆疊高度。 | dp, dag-longest-path | [C](UVa-c/volume100/10051_tower-of-cubes.c) |
| UVa 10054. The Necklace | Each bead has two colored ends. Arrange all beads in a closed necklace so touching ends have equal colors, or report that no arrangement using every bead exists. | 項鍊 | 輸入多顆珠子兩端的顏色；每種顏色視為頂點、珠子視為無向邊，必須把所有珠子恰用一次並首尾相接。完整作法須檢查度數與連通條件，再建立歐拉迴路並按順序輸出相接顏色 | graph, eulerian-tour | [C](UVa-c/volume100/10054_the-necklace.c) |
| UVa 10055. Hashmat the Brave Warrior | For every pair of army sizes, print the absolute difference between the two values. Input continues until end of file. | 勇士哈希馬特 | 每行輸入兩支軍隊的人數，資料讀到檔案結尾；任務是計算兩者相差多少。程式使用足以容納題目數值的整數型別，取兩數差的絕對值並逐行輸出。 | math, basic-io, 64-bit-integer | [C](UVa-c/volume100/10055_hashmat-the-brave-warrior.c) |
| UVa 10056. What is the Probability? | N players repeatedly take turns, and each attempt succeeds independently with probability p. Compute the probability that a specified player is the first winner. | 機率是多少？ | 輸入玩家數 N、每次嘗試成功率 p，以及指定玩家的順序；所有人輪流嘗試，第一位成功者獲勝，若一輪全失敗便重新開始。程式把指定玩家在每輪輪到前所需失敗與重複整輪的機率寫成等比級數，輸出其最終勝率。 | math, probability | [C](UVa-c/volume100/10056_what-is-the-probability.c) |
| UVa 10057. A Mid-Summer Night's Dream | For a multiset of integers, characterize every integer A that minimizes the sum of absolute differences. Print the smallest such A, the number of input values lying in the optimal interval, and the number of integer choices for A. | 仲夏夜之夢 | 輸入一組整數；任務是找使所有 \|X_i-A\| 總和最小的整數 A。排序後，奇數筆資料只有中位數最佳，偶數筆則兩個中位數之間的所有整數都最佳；程式輸出最小最佳值、落在最佳區間內的輸入值數量，以及可選 A 的整數個數。 | math, sorting, median | [C](UVa-c/volume100/10057_a-mid-summer-nights-dream.c) |
| UVa 10061. How Many Zeros and How Many Digits? | For N! represented in base B, compute the number of trailing zero digits and the total number of digits. Print both values for each pair N and B. | 有幾個尾零與幾位數？ | 輸入 N 與進位基底 B；要計算 N! 在 B 進位表示下末尾有幾個零，以及總共有幾位。程式分解 B 的質因數並用勒讓德公式找 N! 中各因子的數量，尾零取可組成 B 的最少組數；位數則由對數和求得並輸出兩值。 | math, number-theory, legendre-formula | [C](UVa-c/volume100/10061_how-many-zeros-and-how-many-digits.c) |
| UVa 10062. Tell Me the Frequencies! | For each input line, count the frequency of every character. | UVa 10063. Knuth's Permutation | Generate all permutations of a string by successively inserting each character into all possible positions. | 高德納全排列產生法 | 依照高德納算法，依序將字串中的每個字元插入到目前排列的所有可能位置，輸出全排列。 | recursion, permutations | [C](UVa-c/volume100/10063_knuths_permutation.c) |
| UVa 10065. Useless Tile Packers | Calculate percentage of wasted space when packing a polygon tile into a container shaped as its convex hull. | 無用瓷磚包裝 (多邊形與凸包面積浪費率) | 計算將任意形狀的瓷磚放入其凸包形狀的包裝盒時，所浪費空間的百分比 ((凸包面積 - 瓷磚面積) / 凸包面積 * 100%)。 | geometry, convex-hull, polygon-area | [C](UVa-c/volume100/10065_useless-tile-packers.c) |

Find the maximum number of tiles that can be kept in the same relative order in both towers. | 雙塔 | 輸入兩座塔由下而上或依序排列的磁磚編號；任務是在不改變各自相對順序下，找出兩序列可共同保留的最大磁磚數。程式建立經典 LCS 二維 DP，輸出每組案例的最大共同子序列長度。 | dynamic-programming, lcs | [C](UVa-c/volume100/10066_the-twin-towers.c) |

| UVa 10071. Back to High School Physics | Given the velocity v at time t for a symmetric constant-acceleration motion setup, compute the displacement over the corresponding interval 2t as 2vt. | 重返高中物理 | 每行輸入速度 v 與時間 t；依題目所描述的等加速度關係，要求從對稱時間區間得到位移。程式直接套用 S = 2vt，使用整數運算並逐行輸出位移。 | math, physics | [C](UVa-c/volume100/10071_back-to-high-school-physics.c) |
| UVa 10074. Take the Land | Given a binary matrix, find the largest axis-aligned rectangular area containing only zero-valued cells and print its area. | 攻占土地 | 輸入由 0 與 1 組成的矩陣；要找完全由 0 構成、邊與矩陣平行的最大矩形。程式逐列累積每欄連續零的高度，把每列轉成最大直方圖矩形問題並以單調堆疊求解，最後輸出全域最大面積。 | dynamic-programming, monotonic-stack, matrix | [C](UVa-c/volume100/10074_take-the-land.c) |

The Stern-Brocot Number System | Find the path of Left ('L') and Right ('R') branch choices in the Stern-Brocot tree to reach a given fraction M/N. | Stern-Brocot 樹二分查找路徑 | 給定最簡真分數或假分數 M/N，求出在 Stern-Brocot 二元樹中自根節點 1/1 到達該分數的左右分支出路徑 (L/R 字串)。 | binary-search, math | [C](UVa-c/volume100/10077_the-stern-brocot-number-system.c) |
| UVa 10078. The Art Gallery | Determine if a polygon representing an art gallery has critical points (not completely convex). | 美術館警戒盲點 (多邊形凸性判定) | 判定表示美術館平面的多邊形是否不是嚴格凸多邊形（即是否存在視覺盲點的臨界點）。 | geometry, convex-polygon, cross-product | [C](UVa-c/volume100/10078_the-art-gallery.c) |

Pizza Cutting | Determine the maximum number of regions into which a pizza can be divided by N straight cuts, assuming cuts are placed optimally. | 切披薩 | 輸入直線切割次數 N；每條新切線若與先前切線在披薩內各自相交且沒有三線共點，就能新增最多區塊。程式使用最大區塊數公式 N(N+1)/2+1，以寬整數計算並輸出答案。 | math | [C](UVa-c/volume100/10079_pizza-cutting.c) |

| UVa 10082. WERTYU | A typist's hands were shifted one key to the right on a QWERTY keyboard. | UVa 10083. Division | For given t, a, and b, determine whether (t^a - 1) / (t^b - 1) is an integer with fewer than 100 decimal digits, and print the value or the required diagnostic message. | 除法 | 輸入 t、a、b，考察幾何級數形式 (t^a-1)/(t^b-1)。程式先以 a 是否可被 b 整除判斷結果是否為整數，再用對數估計位數是否達到 100；只有通過條件才計算並輸出值，否則輸出題目指定訊息。 | math, number-theory | [C](UVa-c/volume100/10083_division.c) |

| UVa 10093. An Easy Problem! | Given a number written with digits 0-9, A-Z, and a-z, find the smallest base from 2 through 62 in which the represented value is divisible by base minus one, or report that no base works. | 一個簡單問題！ | 輸入使用數字與大小寫英文字母表示的數字字串；每個字元對應 0 至 61，因此候選基底必須大於最大數位值。利用一個數對 base-1 的餘數等於數位和餘數，程式由最小合法基底逐一檢查並輸出第一個成功者，皆失敗則輸出不可能訊息。 | math, number-theory, base-conversion | [C](UVa-c/volume100/10093_an-easy-problem.c) |
| UVa 10098. Generating Fast, Sorted Permutation | Generate every distinct permutation of each input string in lexicographic order, including correct handling of repeated characters. | 快速產生排序排列 | 輸入多個字串；每個字串都要列出所有不重複排列，並按字典序輸出。程式先排序字元得到最小排列，再反覆尋找右側遞減區、交換樞紐與下一個較大字元並反轉後綴，以 next permutation 依序產生結果。 | backtracking, string, permutation | [C](UVa-c/volume100/10098_generating-fast.c) |
| UVa 10099. The Tourist Guide | Roads between cities have passenger capacities. Find a route maximizing the minimum road capacity, reserve one seat for the guide, and compute the minimum trips needed to carry all tourists. | 觀光導遊 | 輸入城市道路及每條道路的載客上限，再給起點、終點與旅客人數。程式用 max-min Floyd–Warshall 求兩地間瓶頸容量最大的路徑；每車須保留一個導遊座位，因此以容量減一後向上取整，輸出最少趟數。 | graph, shortest-path, floyd-warshall | [C](UVa-c/volume100/10099_the-tourist-guide.c) |

Bangla Numbers | Convert each nonnegative integer to the Bangla numbering system, using kuti, lakh, hajar, and shata at their proper place values while preserving the required case numbering format. | 孟加拉數字 | 輸入多個非負整數；任務是依孟加拉計數單位把數字拆成 kuti、lakh、hajar、shata 與剩餘部分。程式由大單位遞迴分解，特別處理 kuti 可重複出現的層級，並在每行輸出案例編號與格式化結果。 | math, recursion, simulation | [C](UVa-c/volume101/10101_bangla-numbers.c) |

| UVa 10105. Polynomial Coefficients | Given n and k exponents n_1, n_2, ..., n_k such that sum(n_i) = n, calculate the multinomial coefficient n! / (n_1! * n_2! * ... * n_k!). | 多元多項式展開項係數計算 | 給定總次數 n 與 k 個項的次數 n_1, ..., n_k (滿足總和為 n)，計算多項式定理展開項之係數 n! / (n_1! * ... * n_k!)。 | math, combinatorics | [C](UVa-c/volume101/10105_polynomial-coefficients.c) |
| UVa 10106. Product | Multiply two non-negative integers up to 250 digits each. | 高精度大數乘法 | 精確計算兩個長達 250 位數的非負大整數乘積。 | math, big-integer | [C](UVa-c/volume101/10106_product-bigint.c) |
| UVa 10107. What is the Median? | Integers arrive one at a time. After every insertion, print the median of all values read so far; for an even count, use the integer average of the two middle values. | 中位數是多少？ | 輸入是一串讀到檔案結尾的整數；每讀入一個新值，都要立刻輸出目前全部資料的中位數。程式維持有序陣列並把新值插入正確位置；筆數為奇數時取中央值，偶數時取兩個中央值的整數平均。 | sorting, data-structures, median | [C](UVa-c/volume101/10107_what-is-the-median.c) |
| UVa 10110. Light, More Light | Bulb n is toggled once for every positive divisor of n. Determine whether it is on after all passes, for each input value until the terminating zero. | 開燈，再開燈 | 對編號 n 的燈泡，每個能整除 n 的人都會切換一次開關；輸入多個 n 並以 0 結束。一般因數成對出現，只有完全平方數有單獨的平方根因數而被切換奇數次，因此程式判斷 n 是否為完全平方數並輸出 yes 或 no。 | math, number-theory | [C](UVa-c/volume101/10110_light-more-light.c) |
| UVa 10112. Myacm Triangles | Find 3 points forming a triangle of maximum area that contains no other points strictly inside or on boundaries. | 最大無遮擋三角形 | 在給定點集中尋找面積最大的三角形，使得該三角形內部及邊界上不包含點集中的任何其他點。 | geometry, brute-force | [C](UVa-c/volume101/10112_myacm-triangles.c) |
| UVa 10114. Loansome Car Buyer | Simulate monthly car depreciation and loan repayment to find the first month where the car value exceeds the remaining loan amount. | 買車貸款折舊與負債平衡月數 | 模擬每月車輛折舊與本金分期攤還，求出首個使汽車剩餘價值大於未償還貸款金額的月份。 | simulation | [C](UVa-c/volume101/10114_loansome-car-buyer.c) |
| UVa 10115. Automatic Editing | Apply replacement rules to a text in the given order. For each rule, repeatedly replace the leftmost occurrence of its search string until no occurrence remains, then continue with the next rule and print the final text. | 自動編輯 | 輸入多組搜尋字串與替換字串，接著輸入待編輯文字；規則必須依序執行，同一規則要持續替換最左邊匹配處，直到找不到再換下一條，最後輸出完成文字。 | string, simulation | [C](UVa-c/volume101/10115_automatic-editing.c) |
| UVa 10116. Robot Motion | Simulate robot following grid instructions (N, S, E, W). Output either steps to exit grid or steps before entering a cycle and cycle length. | 網格機器人移動迴圈環路檢測 | 在給定方位指示 (N, S, E, W) 的網格中模擬機器人移動，輸出成功脫離網格的步數，或陷入環路前的步數與環長度。 | simulation, 2d-grid | [C](UVa-c/volume101/10116_robot-motion.c) |
| UVa 10125. Sumsets | Find largest d in set S such that a + b + c = d for distinct a, b, c, d. | 四數和等式最大目標值 (a + b + c = d) | 從給定整數集合中尋找最大的元素 d，滿足存在集合中另三個相異元素使得 a + b + c = d。 | sorting, two-pointers, meet-in-the-middle | [C](UVa-c/volume101/10125_sumsets.c) |
| UVa 10127. Ones | For each input integer n, find the smallest length of a decimal number consisting only of digit 1 that is divisible by n. | 全 1 數 | 每行輸入整數 n；要找最短的 1、11、111……使其可被 n 整除，並輸出這個全 1 數的位數。程式不實際建立巨大整數，而用 'remainder = (remainder×10+1) mod n' 逐位更新，首次餘數為 0 時即得到答案。 | math, number-theory | [C](UVa-c/volume101/10127_ones.c) |
| UVa 10128. Queue | Given N people of distinct heights standing in a line, count permutations where P people are visible from the front and R from the back. | 隊列視角 (斯特林數與排列DP) | N 個人排成一列，求由前看可見 P 個人、由後看可見 R 個人的排列總數。 | dp, combinatorics, stirling-numbers | [C](UVa-c/volume101/10128_queue.c) |
| UVa 10129. Play on Words | Determine whether all given words can be ordered so that the final letter of each word equals the first letter of the next. This is an Euler-trail test on a directed graph of letters. | 文字接龍 | 輸入多個單字；把每個單字視為從首字母指向尾字母的一條有向邊，問題是能否把每條邊恰用一次排成接龍。完整判定需同時檢查入度與出度差，以及所有有邊字母的弱連通性，最後輸出能否開門。 | graph, eulerian-path, dsu | [C](UVa-c/volume101/10129_play-on-words.c) |
| UVa 10130. SuperSale | Items have prices and weights, and each family member has an individual carrying capacity. Find the maximum total value each person can carry and print the sum over all family members. | 超級特賣 | 輸入商品的價值與重量，再輸入每位家庭成員可承受的重量上限；同一成員至多取每件商品一次，但不同成員的選擇彼此獨立。程式先對最大容量做一次 0/1 背包，之後查表加總每位成員的最佳價值並輸出。 | dynamic-programming, knapsack | [C](UVa-c/volume101/10130_supersale.c) |
| UVa 10131. Is Bigger Smarter? | Each elephant has a weight and an IQ. Find the longest sequence in which weights strictly increase while IQ values strictly decrease, and print the original indices of the elephants in order. | 越大越聰明嗎？ | 輸入若干大象的體重與智商直到檔案結尾；要找最長序列，使體重嚴格增加、智商嚴格下降。程式保留原輸入編號，排序後以 DP 求最長合法前驅鏈，並由 parent 指標回溯，輸出長度與各大象原編號。 | dynamic-programming, lis, sorting | [C](UVa-c/volume101/10131_is-bigger-smarter.c) |
| UVa 10132. File Fragmentation | A file was fragmented into 2N pieces (each file split into 2 fragments). | UVa 10137. The Trip | Students paid different amounts on a trip. | UVa 10139. Factovisors | Determine whether integer M divides N! (N factorial). | 階乘被除數整除性勒讓德檢驗 | 判定整數 M 是否能整除 N! (N 的階乘)。 | math, number-theory | [C](UVa-c/volume101/10139_factovisors.c) |
| UVa 10140. Prime Distance | Within each inclusive interval [L, U], find the pair of adjacent primes with the smallest gap and the pair with the largest gap. | UVa 10142. Australian Voting | Simulate Australian preferential voting: count 1st preferences; if no candidate has > 50%, eliminate candidate(s) with lowest votes and redistribute. | 澳洲排序複選制即時決選投票 | 模擬澳洲偏好投票制 (排序複選制)：統計首選票數，若無人過半則淘汰最低得票者並將選票依序遞補，重複直到勝出或平手。 | simulation | [C](UVa-c/volume101/10142_australian-voting.c) |
| UVa 10147. Highways | Given town locations and already existing highways, find additional highways to build to connect all towns with minimum total length. | 公路系統建設 | 給定城鎮座標與已建成的公路，求需額外興建哪些公路以最小總成本使所有城鎮連通。 | graph, mst, kruskal | [C](UVa-c/volume101/10147_highways.c) |

The Lonesome Cargo Distributor | Simulate a cargo carrier moving circularly between N stations with a cargo stack of capacity S and station queues of capacity Q until all cargo is delivered. | 孤獨的貨物搬運工 (環形裝卸模擬) | 搬運車在 N 個環形站點間循環移動，車上有容量為 S 的堆疊，各站有容量為 Q 的隊列，求送完所有貨物的最少時間。 | simulation, queue, stack | [C](UVa-c/volume101/10172_the-lonesome-cargo-distributor.c) |

(2/3/4)-D Sqr/Rects/Cubes/Boxes? | Count squares vs non-square rectangles in 2D, cubes vs non-cube boxes in 3D, and hypercubes vs hyperboxes in 4D of an N-grid. | 多維正方體與長方體計數 | 在邊長為 N 的網格中，分別統計 2D 正方形與非正方形矩形、3D 立方體與非立方體長方體、4D 超立方體與非超立方體總數。 | math, combinatorics | [C](UVa-c/volume101/10177_2-3-4-d-sqr-rects-cubes-boxes.c) |

Minesweeper | Given mine locations in a rectangular field, replace every non-mine cell with the number of mines among its eight neighboring cells, preserving mines and printing each numbered field. | 踩地雷 | 輸入多個由 '*' 與 '.' 組成的雷區，直到尺寸 0 0；每個非地雷格要填入周圍八格中地雷的數量，地雷格保留星號。程式逐格檢查合法鄰座標並累計，依案例格式輸出完成的提示圖。 | 2d-array, simulation, grid | [C](UVa-c/volume101/10189_minesweeper.c) |

Prime Time | For every inclusive interval [a, b], evaluate n²+n+41 at each integer n and print the percentage of those values that are prime, rounded in the required format. | 質數時刻 | 每筆輸入區間端點 a、b；要對其中每個整數 n 計算 n²+n+41，統計結果為質數的比例並輸出百分比。完整作法可先預計算各 n 的質數性與前綴計數，使每個區間能快速取得質數個數 | math, number-theory, prime | [C](UVa-c/volume102/10200_prime-time.c) |

Find the Ways! | Given n and k, determine how many decimal digits are needed to write the binomial coefficient C(n, k), without constructing the potentially enormous coefficient. | 找出方法數 | 每行輸入 n、k；任務是輸出組合數 C(n,k) 的十進位位數。程式利用對稱性取較小的 k，累加分子與分母的常用對數，最後以總和向下取整再加一得到位數，避免直接計算巨大整數。 | math, combinatorics | [C](UVa-c/volume102/10219_find-the-ways-.c) |

Compute each farmer's premium as the farm size multiplied by the rating, sum these premiums, and output the total. | 生態獎金 | 每組輸入會給出多位農民，以及各自的農地面積、動物數量與環保等級。題目要求把每位農民的面積乘上環保等級，再將所有補助金相加，輸出該組資料的補助總額；動物數在原公式中會互相消去。 | math, elementary | [C](UVa-c/volume103/10300_ecological-premium.c) |
