| UVa 10302. Summation of Polynomials | Compute the sum of cubes 1^3 + 2^3 + ... + x^3 for a given integer x. | 立方和多項式級數求和 | 給定整數 x，計算前 x 個正整數的立方和 1^3 + 2^3 + ... + x^3。 | math | [C](UVa-c/volume103/10302_summation-of-polynomials.c) |
| UVa 10303. How Many Trees? | Calculate the number of structurally unique binary search trees that can store 1..n keys: Catalan(n) = C(2n, n) / (n + 1). | 多少棵二元搜尋樹？ (大數卡特蘭數) | 計算由 1..n 鍵值可構成的相異二元搜尋樹數量：即第 n 項卡特蘭數 C_n = C(2n, n) / (n+1)。 | math, catalan-numbers, big-integer | [C](UVa-c/volume103/10303_how-many-trees.c) |
| UVa 10304. Optimal Binary Search Tree | Find the minimum search cost of an optimal binary search tree for a sequence of keys with access frequencies. | 最優二元搜尋樹 (區間動態規劃) | 給定鍵值的訪問頻率，構建最優二元搜尋樹以最小化檢索所有鍵值的總比較代價。 | dp, interval-dp, knuth-optimization | [C](UVa-c/volume103/10304_optimal-binary-search-tree.c) |
| UVa 10305. Ordering Tasks | The input gives a set of tasks and directed precedence relations, where one task must be completed before another. Produce and output any ordering that satisfies every relation. | 工作排序 | 輸入包含若干工作與有向的先後限制，每條限制表示某項工作必須在另一項工作之前完成。需要對這張有向圖做拓撲排序，輸出任一個符合全部限制的工作執行順序。 | graph, topological-sort, dfs | [C](UVa-c/volume103/10305_ordering-tasks.c) |
| UVa 10306. e-Coins | Each coin contributes a pair of integer values. From the available coin types, choose coins with repetition so that the sums of the two coordinates satisfy x² + y² = S². Output the minimum number of coins, or report that no such combination exists. | 電子硬幣 | 輸入給出多種電子硬幣，每種硬幣具有一對整數值，且每種可以重複使用。要讓所選硬幣在兩個座標上的總和 '(x, y)' 滿足 'x² + y² = S²'，輸出需要的最少硬幣數；若無法達成則輸出題目指定的無解訊息。 | dynamic-programming, knapsack | [C](UVa-c/volume103/10306_e-coins.c) |
| UVa 10310. Dog and Gopher | Determine if a gopher can reach a gopher hole before a dog catches it (dog runs twice as fast as the gopher). | 地鼠與獵犬逃生地洞距離幾何判斷 | 獵犬奔跑速度為地鼠的兩倍，檢驗地鼠是否能在獵犬攔截前逃入某個地洞 (即 2 * dist(gopher, hole) <= dist(dog, hole))。 | geometry | [C](UVa-c/volume103/10310_dog-and-gopher.c) |
| UVa 10311. Goldbach and Euler | Determine if integer n can be expressed as the sum of two distinct primes p1 < p2 such that p2 - p1 is minimized. | 哥德巴赫與尤拉 (嚴格兩相異質數和) | 判定整數 n 是否能表示為兩個嚴格相異質數之和 p1 + p2 = n (p1 < p2)，若有多解使 p2 - p1 最小。 | math, number-theory, prime | [C](UVa-c/volume103/10311_goldbach-and-euler.c) |
| UVa 10313. Pay the Price | Count integer partitions of N using parts 1..N with constraints on the number of parts used (at most L2, or between L1 and L2). | 付出代價 (硬幣數目限制的整數拆分DP) | 求將整數 N 拆分為不超過 N 的正整數之和的拆分數，且限制使用的硬幣/拆分數目在給定區間內。 | dp, integer-partition | [C](UVa-c/volume103/10313_pay-the-price.c) |

| UVa 10323. Factorial! You Must be Kidding!!! | Compute n! if it fits between 10,000 and 6,227,020,800. | UVa 10324. Zeros and Ones | Determine whether all characters in string between indices min(i, j) and max(i, j) are identical. | 0與1區間一致性查詢 | 給定由 '0' 與 '1' 組成的字串，多次查詢區間 [min(i, j), max(i, j)] 內的所有字元是否完全相同。 | string, prefix-sum | [C](UVa-c/volume103/10324_zeros-and-ones.c) |

The Playboy Chimp | Given heights of N female chimps sorted in non-decreasing order, find the tallest chimp strictly shorter than Q and shortest chimp strictly taller than Q. | 花花公子黑猩猩身高二分查找 | 給定由小到大排列的 N 隻母猩猩身高，對於查詢身高 Q，找出嚴格小於 Q 的最高者與嚴格大於 Q 的最矮者。 | binary-search | [C](UVa-c/volume106/10611_the-playboy-chimp.c) |

For each pair of coordinates, map both points to their sequence indices and output the number of steps from the first point to the second. | 你能解出來嗎？ | 平面上的整數座標依斜對角線順序編號，輸入每組的起點 '(x1, y1)' 與終點 '(x2, y2)'。利用 's=x+y' 的三角數公式求出兩點編號，再輸出從起點走到終點所需的步數差。 | math, geometry | [C](UVa-c/volume106/10642_can-you-solve-it.c) |
| UVa 10646. What is the Card? | Simulate card magic trick: 52-card deck. Top 25 cards held in hand. 3 times execute X = value(top), remove (10 - X) cards, add X to Y. | UVa 10651. Pebble Solitaire | A 12-hole board has pebbles. A pebble can jump over an adjacent pebble into an empty hole, removing the jumped pebble. Count inversions during merge sort and output the 64-bit total for each sequence. | 超快速排序 | 每組輸入是一個數列，要計算透過相鄰元素交換完成排序所需的最少交換次數。此數量等於逆序對總數，在合併排序合併左右半部時累加跨區逆序對，最後輸出可能很大的計數。 | sorting, merge-sort, inversion-number | [C](UVa-c/volume108/10810_ultra-quicksort.c) |

Recover the larger and smaller scores and output them, or output 'impossible' when no valid integer pair exists. | 破解分差 | 每組輸入給兩個非負整數比分的總和 's' 與差 'd'。解聯立式得到較大值 '(s+d)/2' 與較小值 '(s-d)/2'；若 's<d' 或奇偶性使結果不是整數，則輸出 'impossible'，否則輸出兩個比分。 | math | [C](UVa-c/volume108/10812_beat-the-spread.c) |
| UVa 10813. Traditional BINGO | Simulate calling 75 balls on a 5x5 Bingo card (center is free). Determine after how many calls a full line is completed. | 傳統賓果遊戲模擬 | 模擬 5x5 賓果卡（中心為已標記的免費格），依搖號序列叫號，求在第幾次叫號時最先連成一條線（行、列或對角線）。 | simulation, grid | [C](UVa-c/volume108/10813_traditional-bingo.c) |
| UVa 10814. Simplifying Fractions | Simplify a fraction P / Q with large integer numerator and denominator to lowest terms. | 大數分數約分 (大數GCD) | 將大整數分子 P 與大整數分母 Q 組成的分數約分為最簡分數。 | math, big-integer, gcd | [C](UVa-c/volume108/10814_simplifying-fractions.c) |
| UVa 10815. Andy's First Dictionary | Read arbitrary text, treating every maximal run of letters as a word. | UVa 10819. Trouble of 13-Dots | Buy items to maximize total favour within budget M. | UVa 10978. Let's Play Magic! | Reconstruct the initial deck arrangement of N cards from a sequence of (card, word) pairs where each card is placed after counting empty slots by word length. | 玩魔術！ (拼詞數牌撲克牌還原) | 根據依單字字母長度數空卡槽放置卡片的過程，還原初始 N 張撲克牌的擺放順序。 | simulation, array | [C](UVa-c/volume109/10978_lets-play-magic.c) |
| UVa 10986. Sending email | Find the minimum latency path to route an email from source server S to destination server T in an undirected network. | 伺服器郵件路由最短延遲時間 | 在帶有權重的無向伺服器網路中，求將電子郵件自來源主機 S 傳送至目標主機 T 的最短延遲時間。 | graph, dijkstra, shortest-path | [C](UVa-c/volume109/10986_sending-email.c) |
| UVa 10991. Region | Calculate the area of the region bounded by three pairwise mutually externally tangent circles. | 三圓相切封閉區域面積 | 給定三個兩兩外切圓的半徑 r1, r2, r3，計算由三圓所圍出的中央封閉區域面積。 | geometry, trigonometry, area | [C](UVa-c/volume109/10991_region.c) |
| UVa 10994. Simple Addition | Function F(n) = n % 10 if n % 10 > 0, else F(n / 10). Compute sum of F(i) for i from p to q. | 簡單加法數列 (末位非零數遞歸求和) | 函數 F(n)：若末位大於 0 則為末位數字，否則為 F(n/10)。求 F(i) 在區間 [p, q] 上的總和。 | math, recursion | [C](UVa-c/volume109/10994_simple-addition.c) |
| UVa 11000. Bee | Compute number of male bees and total bees in year N given reproduction biology rules. | 蜜蜂王國繁衍 (費氏數列 DP 規律) | 公蜂生下一公一母，母蜂只生一公，母蜂不死。求第 N 年時公蜂數量與全部蜜蜂總數 (公蜂為 F(N+2) - 1)。 | dynamic-programming, math, fibonacci | [C](UVa-c/volume110/11000_bee.c) |
| UVa 11001. Necklace | Given total clay volume V and threshold volume V_0, divide into b identical beads of volume V/b. Bead diameter D = 0.3 * sqrt(V/b - V_0). Maximize total length b * D. | 項鍊 (黏土製珠最大長度) | 將體積為 V 的黏土分為 b 顆珠子（每顆體積 V/b），珠子直徑 D = 0.3 * sqrt(V/b - V_0)，求使項鍊總長度 b * D 最大的劃分數 b。 | math, calculus, extreme-value | [C](UVa-c/volume110/11001_necklace.c) |
| UVa 11003. Boxes | Stack boxes of given weights and load capacities from top to bottom such that each box supports the total weight of all boxes above it. Maximize count. | 箱子堆疊 (重量與承重極限DP) | 依順序自頂向下疊放箱子，每隻箱子所承載的上方箱子總重量不可超過其負重上限，求最多能疊放的箱子數量。 | dp, knapsack, lis | [C](UVa-c/volume110/11003_boxes.c) |
| UVa 11005. Cheapest Base | The input assigns a printing cost to each digit value from 0 to 35, followed by query integers. For every query, evaluate its representation in each base from 2 through 36 and output all bases having the minimum total digit cost. | 最便宜的進位制 | 每組資料先給數值 0 到 35 各自的印刷成本，再給若干待查整數。對每個整數枚舉 2 到 36 進位，反覆取餘數以加總每一位的成本，找出最低成本並輸出所有同樣達到最低值的進位制。 | math, base-conversion | [C](UVa-c/volume110/11005_cheapest-base.c) |
| UVa 11015. 05-2 Rendezvous | Find the person's location to hold a rendezvous party such that the sum of travel distances of all friends is minimized. | 聚會地點選址 (全源最短路徑中心點) | 求使所有朋友前往集合地點的總路程之和最小的聚會成員所在位置。 | graph, floyd-warshall | [C](UVa-c/volume110/11015_05-2-rendezvous.c) |
| UVa 11029. Leading and Trailing | Find the first 3 digits and the last 3 digits of n^k. | 前三位與後三位數字 (快速冪與對數) | 給定底數 n 與指數 k，求 n^k 的前三位數字與後三位數字。 | math, fast-exponentiation, logarithms | [C](UVa-c/volume110/11029_leading-and-trailing.c) |
| UVa 11034. Ferry Loading IV | Given ferry length L meters and cars queuing at left and right banks, find minimum river crossings to transport all cars. | 渡輪載運雙岸排隊橫渡次數 | 給定長度 L 公尺的渡輪與左右兩岸各自排隊的車輛長度，求運送完所有車輛所需之最少渡河次數。 | queue, greedy, simulation | [C](UVa-c/volume110/11034_ferry-loading-iv.c) |
| UVa 11039. Building designing | Construct a tower of maximum floors with alternating colors (positive = blue, negative = red) of strictly decreasing absolute sizes. | 大樓設計 | 用大小遞減且紅藍兩色交替的樓層（正數為藍，負數為紅）堆疊出最高的大樓。 | greedy, sorting | [C](UVa-c/volume110/11039_building-designing.c) |
| UVa 11040. Add bricks in the wall | A triangular wall of 9 rows has bricks satisfying brick[r][c] = brick[r+1][c] + brick[r+1][c+1]. | UVa 11044. Searching for Nessy | Cover an n x m grid with 3 x 3 sonars avoiding edges. Find minimum sonars needed. | 搜尋尼斯水怪 (聲納雷達網格覆蓋) | 在 n x m 的湖泊網格中安裝 3x3 聲納 (邊界一圈不需覆蓋)，求覆蓋全湖所需的最少聲納數量。 | math | [C](UVa-c/volume110/11044_searching-for-nessy.c) |
| UVa 11045. My T-shirt suits me | Distribute N shirts equally among 6 sizes (N/6 each) to M volunteers who each accept 2 specific sizes. | 衣服尺碼分配 (二分圖最大匹配) | 有 6 種尺寸的 T-shirt 共 N 件（每種尺寸 N/6 件），分配給 M 位志工（每位有 2 種合身尺寸），判定是否能全員滿足。 | bipartite-matching, max-flow | [C](UVa-c/volume110/11045_my-t-shirt-suits-me.c) |
| UVa 11054. Wine trading in Gergovia | N inhabitants on a street want to buy (>0) or sell (<0) wine (sum = 0). Moving 1 unit of wine 1 house costs 1 work unit. Find minimum total work. | 格爾戈維亞葡萄酒交易最小運輸勞動量 | 一條街上 N 戶人家各自買進 (>0) 或賣出 (<0) 葡萄酒 (總需供平衡為 0)，每運送 1 桶酒 1 戶距離耗費 1 單位工作量，求最小總工作量。 | greedy | [C](UVa-c/volume110/11054_wine-trading-in-gergovia.c) |
| UVa 11057. Exact Sum | Find pair of book prices summing to M with minimum difference between prices. | 精準買書金額配對 (雙指標差值最小化) | 從書籍價格清單中挑選兩本書，總價格恰等於 M，且兩本書價差最小 (排序後雙指標搜尋)。 | two-pointers, sorting, binary-search | [C](UVa-c/volume110/11057_exact-sum.c) |

Beverages | Given N beverages in original input order and precedence rules, output a legal drinking sequence respecting priority (Kahn's topological sort). | 飲品偏好偏序關係拓撲排序 | 給定飲料初始偏好順序與飲用先後依賴關係，輸出滿足依賴約束且優先維持原輸入順序之合法飲用序列 (Kahn 拓撲排序)。 | graph, topological-sort | [C](UVa-c/volume110/11060_beverages.c) |

Place the Guards | Place minimum guards on intersections such that every street has at least one guard and no two guards share a street (Bipartite graph vertex cover). | 街道守衛二分圖二著色最小守衛數 | 在街道交會點佈置最少數量的警衛，使得每條街均受到巡查且相鄰兩點不可同時設哨 (二分圖二著色最小頂點覆蓋)。 | graph, bipartite | [C](UVa-c/volume110/11080_place-the-guards.c) |

Generalized Matrioshkas | Verify if a sequence of negative (open) and positive (close) integers represents a valid set of generalized nested matrioshkas. | 廣義俄羅斯套娃 (堆疊配對與容量驗證) | 驗證正負整數序列是否為合法嵌套的俄羅斯套娃（負數為開套娃，正數為封套娃，內部直接包含的套娃尺寸和嚴格小於外層）。 | stack, data-structures | [C](UVa-c/volume111/11111_generalized-matrioshkas.c) |

| UVa 11231. Black and white painting | Count how many 8x8 standard chessboards (bottom-right square white) can be cut out of an n x m grid with top-left square colored c. | 黑白棋盤格 8x8 棋盤子矩陣計數 | 在 n x m 的交錯黑白方格網中，計算有多少個 8x8 標準西洋棋盤 (右下角格子為白色) 可以被完整裁切出來。 | math | [C](UVa-c/volume112/11231_black-and-white-painting.c) |
| UVa 11233. Deli Deli | Transform English nouns into their plural forms given an irregular dictionary and standard morphological rules. | 外送菜單英語複數變形 | 根據不規則名詞字典與標準英語複數形態規則，將單詞轉換為複數形。 | string, rule-based-simulation | [C](UVa-c/volume112/11233_deli-deli.c) |
| UVa 11235. Frequent values | Given a non-decreasing sorted array, find the maximum frequency of any value in query range [L, R]. | 區間最高頻率連續值統計 | 給定一個非遞減排序整數陣列，求指定區間 [L, R] 內出現頻率最高之數值的出現次數。 | data-structures, rmq | [C](UVa-c/volume112/11235_frequent-values.c) |
| UVa 11236. Grocery store | Find all combinations of 4 item prices a <= b <= c <= d in cents such that a+b+c+d == a*b*c*d / 10^6. | 雜貨店四件商品總價與總乘積巧合 | 求 4 個商品價格（分）使得四者之和等於四者相乘除以 10^6（即以元為單位的總和等於總乘積）。 | math, brute-force, pruning | [C](UVa-c/volume112/11236_grocery_store.c) |
| UVa 11239. Open Source | Count registered students for open source projects. If a student signs up for multiple projects, disqualify that student from all projects. | 開源專案選拔 (去重與跨專案除名) | 統計各開源專案的報名學生人數；若同一學生報名了超過一個專案，則該學生在所有專案中均被除名失效。 | string, sorting, hash | [C](UVa-c/volume112/11239_open-source.c) |
| UVa 11242. Tour de France | Given teeth count on front and rear sprockets of a bicycle, find the maximum ratio between adjacent drive ratios when sorted. | 環法自行車賽 | 給定自行車前齒輪與後齒輪的齒數，求所有齒輪比排序後，相鄰齒輪比之間的最大比值。 | sorting, simulation | [C](UVa-c/volume112/11242_tour-de-france.c) |
| UVa 11244. Counting Stars | Count the number of lone stars ('*') in a sky grid that have no neighboring '*' in any of the 8 adjacent directions. | 夜空孤立星星計數 | 在星空網格中統計孤立的星星 ('*') 數量：該星號周圍 8 個相鄰格子內均不得出現其他任何星號。 | simulation, 2d-grid | [C](UVa-c/volume112/11244_counting-stars.c) |
| UVa 11258. String Partition | Partition a digit string into integers each <= 2^31 - 1 (2147483647) such that their sum is maximized. | 字串分割求最大 32 位元整數總和 | 將數字字串劃分成多個不超過 32 位元有號整數上限（2147483647）的整數，求劃分後整數總和的最大值。 | dynamic-programming, string | [C](UVa-c/volume112/11258_string_partition.c) |
| UVa 11264. Coin Collector | Find the maximum number of different coins that can be withdrawn from a bank given coins of strictly increasing denominations. | 硬幣收藏家 | 給定嚴格遞增面額的硬幣，求最多可以同時取出多少種不同面額的硬幣。 | greedy | [C](UVa-c/volume112/11264_coin-collector.c) |
| UVa 11286. Conformity | Given course combinations of 5 courses for N students, find the total number of students enrolled in the most popular combination(s). | 選課組合人氣從眾指數統計 | 給定 N 名學生每人選修的 5 門課程編號，找出最受歡迎選課組合中的總修課學生人數。 | sorting, hash | [C](UVa-c/volume112/11286_conformity.c) |
| UVa 11292. Dragon of Loowater | Each knight of height H can cut off a dragon head of diameter <= H and costs H gold. Find minimum gold to slay all heads or report doomed. | 羅特瓦爾的巨龍與英勇騎士 | 身高為 H 的騎士能砍下直徑 <= H 的龍頭並索取 H 枚金幣。求砍下所有龍頭所需的最少金幣總額，若無法斬殺則輸出失敗。 | greedy, sorting | [C](UVa-c/volume112/11292_dragon-of-loowater.c) |
| UVa 11300. Spreading the Wealth | People sitting in a circle each have some gold coins. Find the minimum total amount of transferred coins to make everyone's gold equal. | 財富分配 | 圍成一圈的人各自擁有若干金幣，求使每個人金幣數量相等的最少轉移金幣總量。 | greedy, math, median | [C](UVa-c/volume113/11300_spreading-the-wealth.c) |
| UVa 11313. Gourmet Games | Given n contestants and m contestants participating in each game with 1 winner, determine total games to find a champion, or report if impossible. | 美食大賽淘汰賽輪數 | 給定 n 名選手，每場比賽由 m 人參賽且產生 1 名勝者（淘汰 m-1 人），求決出冠軍所需的總場數，若無法決出則回傳 -1。 | math, simulation | [C](UVa-c/volume113/11313_gourmet_games.c) |
| UVa 11321. Sort! Sort!! and Sort!!! | Sort integers by modulo M ascending. For ties: odd before even; if both odd, larger first; if both even, smaller first. | 奇偶模數複合自訂排序演算法 | 按數字除以 M 的餘數升序排列；若餘數相同：奇數排在偶數前；若同為奇數則較大者在先；若同為偶數則較小者在先。 | sorting | [C](UVa-c/volume113/11321_sort-sort-and-sort.c) |
| UVa 11332. Summing Digits | For every positive integer until the terminating zero, repeatedly replace the value by the sum of its decimal digits until only one digit remains. Output that final digit. | 數字加總 | 逐筆讀入正整數直到 0，將一個數的十進位各位相加；若結果仍有多位數，就繼續做同樣操作，直到只剩一位。輸出最後的數位根，也可用模 9 的公式直接求得。 | math, number-theory | [C](UVa-c/volume113/11332_summing-digits.c) |
| UVa 11340. Newspaper | Given price per character for K paid characters, calculate total cost of an article in dollars and cents. | 報紙字元計費總額計算 | 給定 K 個指定字元的計費單價 (以美分為單位)，計算整篇新聞報導所有字元的總報酬金額。 | string, hash | [C](UVa-c/volume113/11340_newspaper.c) |
| UVa 11342. Three-square | Find three non-negative integers a <= b <= c such that a^2 + b^2 + c^2 = K. | 三平方和定理 | 求三個非負整數 a <= b <= c 使得 a^2 + b^2 + c^2 = K（三平方和）。 | math, number-theory, brute-force | [C](UVa-c/volume113/11342_three-square.c) |
| UVa 11344. The Huge One | Determine if a huge integer M (up to 1000 digits) is divisible by all numbers in a given set S. | 超大整數多整除性檢測 | 給定一個長達 1000 位數的超大整數 M，檢驗其是否能被集合 S 中的所有整數整除。 | math, big-integer | [C](UVa-c/volume113/11344_the-huge-one.c) |
| UVa 11349. Symmetric Matrix | For each square matrix, decide whether every element is non-negative and equals the element mirrored through the matrix center. | UVa 11352. Crazy King | Find the minimum King moves from A to B on a chessboard avoiding enemy knights 'Z' and their attacked squares. | 瘋狂國王 (閃避騎士的最短步數) | 在有敵方騎士 'Z' 的棋盤上，求國王從 'A' 走到 'B' 的最少步數，且不得踩到騎士及其攻擊格。 | graph, bfs, chess | [C](UVa-c/volume113/11352_crazy_king.c) |
| UVa 11360. Have Fun with Matrices | Perform row swaps, col swaps, transpose, and inc/dec modulo 10 on an N x N digit matrix. | 矩陣變換趣味遊戲 | 對 N x N 數字矩陣執行行交換、列交換、轉置、全體加減 1 (模 10) 等操作。 | matrix-simulation | [C](UVa-c/volume113/11360_have-fun-with-matrices.c) |
| UVa 11362. Phone List | Determine whether a list of phone numbers is consistent, meaning no phone number is a prefix of another. | 電話號碼簿前綴無衝突檢驗 | 檢驗電話號碼列表是否合法一致：任一電話號碼均不可為其他號碼的前綴。 | string, sorting | [C](UVa-c/volume113/11362_phone-list.c) |
| UVa 11364. Parking | Find minimum total walking distance to visit all shops along a street and return to car. | 最佳停車地點走動距離最小化 | 在街道一維座標軸上停車，走遍所有商店後回到停車處，求最少總步行距離 (2 * (max - min))。 | math, greedy | [C](UVa-c/volume113/11364_parking.c) |
| UVa 11367. Full Tank? | Find minimum fuel cost to travel between two cities with a fuel tank capacity C, where each city has its own fuel price per liter. | 滿油出發？ (帶油量狀態的最短路Dijkstra) | 給定油箱容量 C 與各城市每升油價，求從起點城市前往終點城市的最小燃油花費。 | graph, dijkstra, shortest-path | [C](UVa-c/volume113/11367_full-tank.c) |
| UVa 11369. Shopaholic | Buy 3 items, get the cheapest free. Maximize total discount by grouping items greedily. | 購物狂買三送一折扣金額最大化 | 商場優惠買三件免一件 (免費贈送三件中最便宜者)，求將所購物品適當分組下能獲得之最大折扣總金額。 | greedy, sorting | [C](UVa-c/volume113/11369_shopaholic.c) |
| UVa 11371. Number Theory for Newbies | Given an integer n, form the largest number by permuting its digits, and the smallest number (no leading zero). | UVa 11384. Help is needed for Dexter | Given a set of numbers 1..N, subtract an integer X from any subset in one move. Find minimum moves to reduce all to 0. | 德克斯特的減半消除 | 對集合 {1, 2, ..., N} 每次可選任意子集減去相同正整數 X，求使所有數變為 0 的最少操作次數。 | math, binary, recursion | [C](UVa-c/volume113/11384_help-is-needed-for-dexter.c) |
| UVa 11385. Da Vinci Code | Given Fibonacci numbers and an encrypted uppercase string, place each character into the 1-based output string at position corresponding to the index of its Fibonacci number. | 達文西密碼 (費氏數列下標解密) | 給定一組費氏數列與大寫英文字串，將各字母放入以其費氏數列項數為下標的輸出字串中，其餘位置填空格。 | math, fibonacci, string | [C](UVa-c/volume113/11385_da-vinci-code.c) |
| UVa 11388. GCD LCM | Given G and L, find two positive integers a <= b such that gcd(a, b) = G and lcm(a, b) = L. | 已知最大公因數與最小公倍數反求二數 | 已知兩整數的 gcd 為 G、lcm 為 L，求滿足條件的最小 a 與相應的 b (若無解輸出 -1)。 | math, number-theory | [C](UVa-c/volume113/11388_gcd-lcm.c) |
| UVa 11389. The Bus Driver Problem | Pair n morning routes and n evening routes to minimize total overtime pay (rate R per hour exceeding d). | 公車司機加班費最佳排班化 | 將 n 條上午路線與 n 條下午路線進行配對，使所有司機超時工作 (超過 d 小時部分每小時給予 R 元加班費) 的總支出達到最小。 | greedy, sorting | [C](UVa-c/volume113/11389_the-bus-driver-problem.c) |
| UVa 11396. Claw Decomposition | Determine if a 3-regular graph can be decomposed into claw graphs (K_{1,3}). | 爪圖分解 (二分圖判定) | 判斷一個 3-正則圖是否能分解為爪圖 (K_{1,3})。由定理知當且僅當圖為二分圖時可分解。 | graph, bipartite-check, 2-coloring | [C](UVa-c/volume113/11396_claw-decomposition.c) |
| UVa 11401. Triangle Counting | Given sticks of integer lengths 1, 2, ..., N, find the number of ways to pick 3 distinct sticks that can form a non-degenerate triangle. | 長度 1 到 N 線段構成三角形組合計數 | 給定長度分別為 1, 2, ..., N 的木棒，求任意挑選三根相異木棒能構成非退化三角形的選法總數。 | math, combinatorics | [C](UVa-c/volume114/11401_triangle-counting.c) |
| UVa 11402. Ahoy, Pirates! | Maintain a binary array up to 1,024,000 pirates supporting set-to-1, set-to-0, flip bits, and count 1s queries. | 海盜！ (線段樹區間賦值與反轉) | 在長度達 1,024,000 的海盜二進位字串上支援區間全設為 1、全設為 0、位元翻轉及求 1 的個數等查詢。 | segment-tree, lazy-propagation | [C](UVa-c/volume114/11402_ahoy-pirates.c) |
| UVa 11407. Squares | Find the minimum number of square numbers required to sum to N. | 平方數最少和 (四平方和定理動態規劃) | 求將正整數 N 表示為完全平方數之和的最少個數（Lagrange 四平方和定理）。 | dp, math | [C](UVa-c/volume114/11407_squares.c) |
| UVa 11413. Fill the Containers | Divide N milk vessels into M containers in sequential order to minimize the maximum container capacity required. | 容器裝水最大容量極小化二分搜尋 | 將 N 杯牛奶依順序倒入 M 個容器中，求所需容器的最大容量之最小值 (二分搜尋 + 貪婪容量檢驗)。 | binary-search, greedy | [C](UVa-c/volume114/11413_fill-the-containers.c) |
| UVa 11417. GCD | For each input 'N' until zero, compute the sum of 'gcd(i, j)' over every pair satisfying '1 ≤ i < j ≤ N'. Output the resulting total. | 最大公因數總和 | 逐筆讀入 'N' 直到 0，枚舉所有滿足 '1 ≤ i < j ≤ N' 的整數對，利用歐幾里得算法求每對的最大公因數並累加，最後輸出總和 'G'。 | math, gcd, number-theory | [C](UVa-c/volume114/11417_gcd.c) |
| UVa 11420. Chest of Drawers | A vertical stack of n drawers can each be locked or unlocked. A drawer is secured if it is locked AND the drawer above it is locked (top drawer is secured if locked). Count ways to have exactly s secured drawers. | 抽屜鎖定問題 (鎖定抽屜計數DP) | n 層抽屜各自可上鎖或不上鎖。抽屜安全當且僅當自身上鎖且其上方抽屜亦上鎖（頂層上鎖即安全），求恰好有 s 個安全抽屜的方案數。 | dp, combinatorics | [C](UVa-c/volume114/11420_chest-of-drawers.c) |
| UVa 11424. GCD - Extreme (I) | Compute sum_{i=1}^{N-1} sum_{j=i+1}^N gcd(i, j) for N up to 200,000. | 極限最大公因數求和 I (尤拉函數倍數和) | 計算 N 以內所有數對的最大公因數總和 G = sum_{i=1}^{N-1} sum_{j=i+1}^N gcd(i, j)（N <= 200,000）。 | math, number-theory, euler-totient | [C](UVa-c/volume114/11424_gcd-extreme-i.c) |
| UVa 11426. GCD - Extreme (II) | Compute G = sum_{i=1}^{N-1} sum_{j=i+1}^N gcd(i, j) for N up to 4000000. | 極限最大公因數求和 (歐拉函數) | 計算 G = sum_{i=1}^{N-1} sum_{j=i+1}^N gcd(i, j)，N 最大達 4000000。 | math, number-theory, euler-totient | [C](UVa-c/volume114/11426_gcd-extreme-ii.c) |
| UVa 11428. Cubes | Given integer N, find smallest integers x > y > 0 such that x^3 - y^3 = N. | 立方之差等式枚舉 (x^3 - y^3 = N) | 給定整數 N，尋找滿足 x^3 - y^3 = N 且 y 最小的正整數解 (x, y)。 | math, brute-force | [C](UVa-c/volume114/11428_cubes.c) |
| UVa 11448. Who said crisis? | Compute difference A - B for two large non-negative integers up to 10,000 digits. | 誰說有危機？ (大數減法) | 計算兩個長達 10,000 位的大整數之差 A - B（若結果為負數需輸出負號）。 | big-integer, math | [C](UVa-c/volume114/11448_who-said-crisis.c) |
| UVa 11450. Wedding shopping | Given budget M and C garment categories, pick one item from each category to maximize total spend <= M, or report no solution. | 婚禮採購預算最大化動態規劃 | 在預算上限 M 內，於 C 類婚禮服飾中每類各挑選一件商品，求總花費小於等於 M 的最大支出金額。 | dynamic-programming | [C](UVa-c/volume114/11450_wedding-shopping.c) |
| UVa 11455. Behold my quadrangle | Given 4 positive segment lengths, classify the quadrangle into square, rectangle, generic quadrangle, or banana (impossible). | 四邊形幾何形態分類判定 | 給定四條正線段長度，判斷其是否可構成正方形、長方形、普通四邊形或根本無法構成四邊形 (banana)。 | geometry | [C](UVa-c/volume114/11455_behold-my-quadrangle.c) |
| UVa 11456. Trainsorting | Train cars arrive sequentially. Each car can be attached to front or back of train. Cars must remain strictly decreasing from front to back. Maximize cars. | 火車車廂雙向掛載最長有序序列 | 車廂依序到達，每節車廂可掛在列車頭部或尾部，整列火車重量必須由頭至尾嚴格遞減。求最多可掛載的車廂節數。 | dynamic-programming, lis | [C](UVa-c/volume114/11456_trainsorting.c) |
| UVa 11459. Snakes and Ladders | Simulate players moving along a 100-cell board with dice rolls, climbing ladders or sliding down snakes until a player reaches 100. | 蛇與梯子棋盤遊戲模擬 | 模擬多名玩家擲骰子在 100 格棋盤上前進，遇到梯子上升、遇到蛇下滑，最先抵達 100 格者獲勝。 | simulation | [C](UVa-c/volume114/11459_snakes-and-ladders.c) |
| UVa 11461. Square Numbers | For each inclusive range '[a, b]' until the terminating pair, count how many integers in the range are perfect squares and output the count. | 平方數 | 每筆輸入給閉區間 '[a, b]'，直到終止資料。要統計區間內有多少完全平方數；由最小可行平方根 'ceil(sqrt(a))' 與最大平方根 'floor(sqrt(b))' 的差即可得到數量，並輸出結果。 | math | [C](UVa-c/volume114/11461_square-numbers.c) |
| UVa 11462. Age Sort | Each data set contains many people's ages from a small fixed range. Sort all ages in nondecreasing order and output them on one line; a frequency array avoids comparison sorting for the large input. | 年齡排序 | 每組輸入包含大量年齡，而年齡只會落在很小的固定範圍。使用頻率陣列統計每個年齡出現次數，再由小到大依次輸出相應次數，即可在線性時間完成非遞減排序。 | sorting, counting-sort | [C](UVa-c/volume114/11462_age-sort.c) |
| UVa 11463. Commandos | Find minimum time for commandos starting at s to visit all buildings and gather at destination d. | 突擊隊 | 突擊隊員從起點 s 出發炸毀所有建築物後在終點 d 集合，求所有人完成任務並會合的最短時間。 | graph, floyd-warshall, shortest-path | [C](UVa-c/volume114/11463_commandos.c) |
| UVa 11464. Even Parity | Given an N x N binary matrix, transform it by flipping only 0s to 1s with minimal flips so every cell has an even number of 1-neighbors. | 偶數奇偶校驗矩陣變換 | 給定一個 N x N 二元矩陣，只能將 0 改為 1，求最少翻轉次數使得每個格子的四鄰居 1 之總數均為偶數。 | bitmask, brute-force, parity | [C](UVa-c/volume114/11464_even_parity.c) |
| UVa 11466. Largest Prime Divisor | Find the largest prime divisor of N, with the requirement that N must have at least two distinct prime divisors; otherwise return -1. | 整數最大質因數 (至少含兩個相異質因數) | 求整數 N 的最大質因數，且 N 必須具有至少兩個相異質因數；若為質數或質數冪則回傳 -1。 | math, number-theory | [C](UVa-c/volume114/11466_largest-prime-divisor.c) |
| UVa 11470. Square Sums | Given an 'N × N' integer matrix, sum the values on each concentric square border, from the outside toward the center. | UVa 11475. Extend to Palindrome | Find the shortest palindrome that can be formed by appending characters to the end of a given string. | 添加最短後綴字元形成迴文 | 在給定字串末尾添加最少數量的字元，使其擴充為一個對稱迴文字串。 | string, kmp | [C](UVa-c/volume114/11475_extend-to-palindrome.c) |

| UVa 11703. sqrt log sin | Compute x_i = (x_{i - sqrt(i)} + x_{ln(i)} + x_{i*sin^2(i)}) mod 1,000,000 with x_0 = 1. | 平方根對數正弦遞推數列 | 計算遞推數列 x_i = (x_{i - sqrt(i)} + x_{ln(i)} + x_{i*sin^2(i)}) mod 1,000,000，初值 x_0 = 1。 | dp, math | [C](UVa-c/volume117/11703_sqrt-log-sin.c) |

| UVa 11715. Car | Compute missing kinematic quantities given 3 out of 5 variables (u, v, t, a, s) under constant acceleration. | 等加速度直線運動物理運動學公式 | 在等加速度直線運動中，已知初速 u、末速 v、時間 t、加速度 a、位移 s 中的三項，求出其餘兩項未知量。 | physics, math | [C](UVa-c/volume117/11715_car.c) |

Numbering Roads | Given R roads and N base numbers with 26 letters (A-Z) available as suffixes, find minimum letters needed (0 to 26), or report impossible. | 道路門牌後綴英文字母指派 | 給定 R 條道路與 N 個基本整數編號，可搭配 26 個英文字母 (A-Z) 作為後綴，求最少需要動用幾個英文字母，若超過 26 則無解。 | math | [C](UVa-c/volume117/11723_numbering-roads.c) |

Find final coordinate. | 機器人移動指令歷史回溯 | 機器人在一維數線上移動，指令可能為 LEFT (-1)、RIGHT (+1) 或 SAME AS i (重複第 i 個指令的動作)。求最後停在的座標位置。 | simulation | [C](UVa-c/volume125/12503_robot-instructions.c) |
| UVa 12504. Updating a Dictionary | Compare an old and new key-value dictionary and output keys that were added (+), removed (-), or modified (*). | 字典更新日誌比對 | 比對舊字典與新字典，列出新增的鍵 (+)、刪除的鍵 (-) 與數值改變的鍵 (*)。 | string, map, parsing | [C](UVa-c/volume125/12504_updating_a_dictionary.c) |
| UVa 12527. Different Digits | Count the number of integers in range [N, M] that have all distinct digits. | 無重複數字計數 | 計算區間 [N, M] 內所有十進位數位互不相同的整數數量。 | math, simulation | [C](UVa-c/volume125/12527_different-digits.c) |
| UVa 12531. Hours and Minutes | Determine if a given angle A (0 <= A <= 180) can ever be formed between the hour hand and minute hand of a clock (discrete 6-degree steps). | 時針與分針夾角判定 | 判斷 0 到 180 度的某個夾角 A 是否可能出現在標準時鐘的時針與分針之間。 | math, clock-angle | [C](UVa-c/volume125/12531_hours-and-minutes.c) |
| UVa 12532. Interval Product | Support point update 'C I V' and range product sign query 'P I J' (+, -, or 0) on an array of integers. | 區間乘積正負號線段樹 / 樹狀陣列 | 維護整數陣列，支援單點修改數值與區間乘積正負號查詢 (輸出 '+', '-' 或 '0')。 | data-structures, segment-tree | [C](UVa-c/volume125/12532_interval-product.c) |
| UVa 12541. Birthdates | Given a list of people with their birth dates, identify the youngest and oldest persons. | 出生日期排序 | 給定一群人的姓名與出生年月日，找出年齡最年輕（出生日期最晚）與最年長（出生日期最早）的人。 | sorting, simulation | [C](UVa-c/volume125/12541_birthdates.c) |
| UVa 12554. A Special Happy Birthday Song | N people sing the 16-word Happy Birthday song in turn. Ensure everyone sings at least once and the entire song is completed in whole rounds. | 生日快樂歌歌詞輪唱循環指派 | N 個人輪流朗唱 16 個單詞的生日快樂歌歌詞，確保每個人至少唱過一次且最後一首歌必須完整唱完 16 句。 | string, simulation | [C](UVa-c/volume125/12554_a-special-happy-birthday-song.c) |
| UVa 12563. Jin Ge Jin Qu hao | Maximize the number of KTV songs sung before time limit t runs out, then maximize total time sung (adding 678s song at end). | 勁歌金曲 (雙目標 0-1 背包問題) | 在 KTV 剩餘時間 t 之前點播歌曲，優先最大化演唱歌曲數，其次最大化總時間，最後加上一首 678 秒的《勁歌金曲》。 | dynamic-programming, knapsack | [C](UVa-c/volume125/12563_jin_ge_jin_qu_hao.c) |

Output 'Hajj-e-Akbar' for 'Hajj' and 'Hajj-e-Asghar' for 'Umrah', including the required case numbering. | 大朝覲 | 逐行讀入朝覲類型，遇到 '*' 結束。若輸入為 'Hajj'，輸出 'Hajj-e-Akbar'；若為 'Umrah'，輸出 'Hajj-e-Asghar'，並依題目格式加上案例編號。 | string | [C](UVa-c/volume125/12577_hajj-e-akbar.c) |

| UVa 12894. Perfect Flag | Check if rectangle and circle conform to Bangladesh flag standard (length=10k, width=6k, radius=2k, center at 45% length). | 完美國旗尺寸與圓心座標檢驗 | 檢驗孟加拉國旗規格：長寬比 10:6，半徑為長度的 1/5，圓心 x 座標位於長度 45% 處、y 座標位於寬度正中央。 | geometry | [C](UVa-c/volume128/12894_perfect-flag.c) |
| UVa 12895. Armstrong Number | Determine if integer is Armstrong number (sum of each digit raised to power of number of digits equals original number). | 阿姆斯壯數判定 (各數位 N 次方和) | 判定整數是否為阿姆斯壯數 (即每位數字的 d 次方之和等於原數，其中 d 為總位數)。 | math | [C](UVa-c/volume128/12895_armstrong-number.c) |
| UVa 12908. The book thief | The sum of page numbers from 1 through an unknown final page is known after one page has been removed. | UVa 12918. Lucky Tracker | Given 'n' keys and 'm' doors, with enough doors for all keys, compute the maximum number of failed trials that can occur before every key is matched to a door. | UVa 12952. Tri-du | Given two cards A and B, determine third card C to maximize probability of getting a Three-of-a-kind or Pair with highest rank. | Tri-du 紙牌遊戲最優第三張牌 | 在 Tri-du 撲克遊戲中已知兩張手牌 A 與 B，挑選第三張牌 C 使牌型最優 (若 A==B 選 A 組成三條，否則選 max(A, B) 組成大對子)。 | math, greedy | [C](UVa-c/volume129/12952_tri-du.c) |
| UVa 12996. Ultimate Mango Challenge | Check if total mangoes eaten <= total limit L and each type eaten <= individual type limit. | 終極芒果大胃王挑戰限制檢驗 | 檢驗大胃王選手吃芒果是否符合規則：吃下的各種類芒果不超過各自上限，且全部芒果總數不超過胃容量 L。 | simulation | [C](UVa-c/volume129/12996_ultimate-mango-challenge.c) |
| UVa 13012. Identifying tea | The input gives the correct tea type followed by five contestants' guesses. | UVa 13025. Back to the Past | Output the fixed date 'May 29, 2013 Wednesday' corresponding to the inaugural contest date. | 重返過去指定歷史星期日期輸出 | 直接印出指定歷史日期 "May 29, 2013 Wednesday"。 | basics | [C](UVa-c/volume130/13025_back-to-the-past.c) |

| UVa 13059. Tournament | Find total matches played in single-elimination tournament of N players until one champion remains. | 單淘汰錦標賽總比賽場次 | N 名選手進行單淘汰錦標賽，每場比賽淘汰 1 人，直到決出唯一的冠軍，求總共需進行幾場比賽。 | math | [C](UVa-c/volume130/13059_tournament.c) |
| UVa 13109. Elephants | Find maximum number of elephants that can stand on a bridge of total capacity W. | 大象過橋最多數量 (貪婪重量排序) | 木橋最大載重為 W，求在不超出橋樑負載限制下，最多能讓多少隻大象同時上橋 (貪婪重量由小到大排序)。 | greedy, sorting | [C](UVa-c/volume131/13109_elephants.c) |
| UVa 13130. Cacho | Determine if 5 dice values form a consecutive straight (Escala) i.e. 1-2-3-4-5 or 2-3-4-5-6. | Cacho 骰子連續順子判定 (Escala) | 判定 5 顆骰子的點數是否形成嚴格公差為 1 的連續順子 (即 a[i] == a[i-1] + 1)。 | simulation | [C](UVa-c/volume131/13130_cacho.c) |
| UVa 13148. A Giveaway | Determine whether a number is 'Special' (both an integer square and an integer cube, i.e., a sixth power). | 雙重冪次判別 (既是平方又是立方) | 判斷給定整數是否為「特殊數」（同時為整數的完全平方與完全立方，即六次方數）。 | math, number-theory | [C](UVa-c/volume131/13148_a_giveaway.c) |
| UVa 13178. Is it multiple of 3? | For each input 'N', consider the huge decimal number formed by concatenating '1, 2, ..., N'. Determine whether that number is divisible by 3 and output the required answer without constructing the concatenation. | 是 3 的倍數嗎？ | 每組輸入給 'N'，題目把 '1、2、…、N' 依序串接成一個可能非常大的十進位數，要求判斷它能否被 3 整除。利用十進位數各位和的整除規則，串接數的數字和等價於 '1' 到 'N' 的數字和，因此依 'N mod 3' 即可輸出答案，無需建立大數。 | math, number-theory | [C](UVa-c/volume131/13178_is-it-multiple-of-3.c) |
| UVa 13185. DPD Numbers | For each positive integer 'N', sum all proper divisors smaller than 'N'. Output 'deficient', 'perfect', or 'abundant' according to whether that sum is less than, equal to, or greater than 'N'. | 不足數、完全數與過剩數 | 每組輸入一個正整數 'N'，找出所有小於 'N' 且能整除 'N' 的真因數並加總。若總和小於 'N'，輸出 'deficient'；等於 'N' 輸出 'perfect'；大於 'N' 則輸出 'abundant'。 | math, number-theory | [C](UVa-c/volume131/13185_dpd-numbers.c) |
| UVa 13190. Rockabye Taby | Simulate medication schedule with periodic reminders. Print next k medication events in chronological order. | 定時服藥排程模擬 (優先佇列) | 模擬多種具有各自週期的藥物服用提醒，依時間順序輸出接下來 k 次服藥提醒的時刻與藥物名稱 (遇同時取優先度高者)。 | data-structures, priority-queue, simulation | [C](UVa-c/volume131/13190_rockabye-taby.c) |
| UVa 13216. Problem with a ridiculously long name... | The exponent 'N' may be too large for an integer type. For each decimal exponent string, output the last two digits of '66^N', including a leading zero when needed, by using the repeating pattern modulo 100. | 名字長得離譜的問題 | 每組輸入是可能極長的十進位指數字串 'N'，要求輸出 '66^N' 的最後兩位，必要時保留前導零。'N=0' 與 'N=1' 分別處理，其餘次方在模 100 下以 5 為週期，只需讀取指數末位即可選出答案。 | math | [C](UVa-c/volume132/13216_problem-with-a-ridiculously-long-name.c) |
| UVa 13271. Brick Walls | Compute shortest path distance between two bricks on a staggered brick wall (honeycomb grid equivalent). | 磚牆六角網格最短步數走訪 | 在錯位排列的磚塊牆面上移動，求從起始磚塊走到目標磚塊所需的最少跳躍步數。 | geometry, math, shortest-path | [C](UVa-c/volume132/13271_brick-walls.c) |
| UVa 13275. Leap Birthdays | Each case gives a birth date and a future year 'Q'. Count how many birthdays can be celebrated after the birth year through 'Q'; ordinary dates occur yearly, while February 29 occurs only in leap years. | 閏日生日 | 每組輸入給出生的日、月、年與目標年份 'Q'，要統計出生年之後到 'Q' 為止能慶祝幾次生日。一般日期每年一次，答案為年份差；若生日是 2 月 29 日，則只計算區間內符合公曆規則的閏年，最後依案例格式輸出次數。 | math | [C](UVa-c/volume132/13275_leap-birthdays.c) |

## 以前比賽的程式（7 題）

| 英文題目名 | 英文描述 | 中文題目名 | 中文詳細描述 | 涵蓋的演算法範圍 | 檔案位置 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Prime Check | Determine whether each input integer is prime. | 質數判斷 | 逐一讀入整數，判斷是否為質數並輸出 Y 或 N。 | 質數、試除法 | [C](%E4%BB%A5%E5%89%8D%E6%AF%94%E8%B3%BD%E7%9A%84code/01_prime_check/solution.c) |
| Roman to Integer | Convert a Roman numeral to an integer. | 羅馬數字轉整數 | 讀入羅馬數字字串，依符號值與減法規則轉為整數。 | 字串、模擬 | [C](%E4%BB%A5%E5%89%8D%E6%AF%94%E8%B3%BD%E7%9A%84code/02_roman_to_integer/solution.c) |
| Fibonacci | Compute a value in the Fibonacci sequence. | 費波那契數列 | 依前兩項之和計算指定的費波那契數。 | 數列、動態規劃 | [C](%E4%BB%A5%E5%89%8D%E6%AF%94%E8%B3%BD%E7%9A%84code/03_fibonacci/solution.c) |
| Fixed Coin Change | Compute change with the available fixed coin denominations. | 固定面額找零 | 依固定硬幣面額拆分指定金額並計算所需硬幣。 | 貪婪法、找零 | [C](%E4%BB%A5%E5%89%8D%E6%AF%94%E8%B3%BD%E7%9A%84code/04_fixed_coin_change/solution.c) |
| Parenthesis Pairs | Process matching pairs of parentheses. | 括號配對 | 處理輸入中的左右括號，依題目規則找出配對關係。 | 字串、堆疊 | [C](%E4%BB%A5%E5%89%8D%E6%AF%94%E8%B3%BD%E7%9A%84code/05_parenthesis_pairs/solution.c) |
| Integer Partitions | Count or enumerate integer partitions. | 整數分拆 | 將整數分拆為若干正整數之和，依題目規則計算結果。 | 動態規劃、組合數 | [C](%E4%BB%A5%E5%89%8D%E6%AF%94%E8%B3%BD%E7%9A%84code/06_integer_partitions/solution.c) |
| Stair Ways 1 2 3 | Count ways to climb stairs using steps of one, two, or three. | 一次走一至三級的爬樓梯方法數 | 每次可走一、二或三級，計算到達指定階數的不同走法數。 | 動態規劃、遞迴 | [C](%E4%BB%A5%E5%89%8D%E6%AF%94%E8%B3%BD%E7%9A%84code/07_stair_ways_123/solution.c) |
